## 2026-05-08 - API-0093
- Implemented coverage for `compose-update` (POST /compose.update) by appending
  one `apiCoverageCase` literal to `coveredAPIOperations` in
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`. This closes out the compose/* tag —
  every compose/* operation declared in `data/openapi.json` is now covered.
- This is the LARGEST request-body schema in the compose/* tag (single
  required `composeId` + 40+ optional siblings spanning every git provider
  knob, build settings, runtime metadata, and refresh-token rotation). The
  closest cross-tag precedent is API-0038 (application-update), the sole
  prior `*-update` entry in the roster, which also ships only the required
  id even though its OpenAPI body declares 30+ optional siblings. Per the
  AGENTS.md "Per-operation API coverage" rule the SampleBody mirrors the
  required-field shape; the bytes go on the wire verbatim, the success
  leg's body-equality check enforces deterministic forwarding, and a
  single-field document is the minimal-but-valid fixture.
- Distinct from the minimal-composeId-only POST family
  (API-0066/0067/0068/0073/0074/0080/0088/0090/0091) — same shape on the
  wire (one required field, empty `{}` 200), but the optional surface in
  the spec is incomparably richer, so naming this entry as the dedicated
  `*-update` twin (rather than collapsing it into the minimal family)
  keeps cross-story grep clean for any future compose/update field
  rotation.
- Files changed:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended one `apiCoverageCase`
    literal at the StoryID-sorted tail (slug
    `compose-cov-update-0093`).
  - `ralph/prd.json` — flipped `passes: true` for API-0093 only.
  - `ralph/progress.txt` — this entry.
- Verification (run from repo root):
  - `gofmt -w .`            → no diff
  - `goimports -w .`        → not installed locally; gofmt sufficient
                              (no imports touched)
  - `go mod tidy`           → no diff
  - `go vet ./...`          → clean
  - `go test ./... -count=1`        → all 9 packages pass
  - `go test -race ./... -count=1`  → all 9 packages pass
  - Targeted: `go test ./internal/cli -run TestAPICoverage -count=1 -v`
    confirms all 5 sub-phases (RegistryInvariants, SchemaCommandWorks,
    ManifestListsOperation, SuccessRoundTrip, RepresentativeFailure)
    PASS for `API-0093/compose-update`.
- Remaining risks / follow-ups: none. With API-0093 the compose/* tag
  is fully covered (API-0066..API-0093, 28 operations). Next priority-2
  story is API-0094 (deployment-all, GET /deployment.all), the first
  entry in the deployment/* tag.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - `*-update` operations consistently mirror the `*-create` body
    schema in field count but downgrade everything except the primary
    id to optional. Across the roster only `composeId`/`applicationId`
    is required. The minimal-but-valid fixture is a single-field
    document, NOT a populated maximum, because the AGENTS.md "Per-
    operation API coverage" rule limits SampleBody to the required-
    shape mirror; populated fixtures are the `*-create` convention
    (API-0014, API-0040, API-0069), not `*-update`.
  - Inside the compose/* tag, distinguish a `*-update` POST from the
    minimal-composeId-only POST family by the optional-surface size,
    not by the wire shape. They serialise identically (`{"composeId":
    "..."}` → `{}` 200) but the spec's optional surface diverges by
    an order of magnitude, and naming the entry as the dedicated
    `*-update` twin keeps the grep map honest.
  - Compose/* tag is now closed (28 operations). The next pending
    priority-2 batch is the deployment/* tag (API-0094..API-0099 in
    the immediate next slice). The deployment/* tag is read-mostly
    (5 GETs, 1 POST in the next slice), so the next API-XXXX iteration
    will likely seed the first deployment/* GET fixture — review
    API-0035 (application-search) and API-0089 (compose-search) for
    the all-optional query-param precedent if applicable.

    "no active organization"), but the harness's representative
    failure leg should still default to 401→CodeAuth. Each per-tag
    opener re-proves the auth invariant; story-specific failure
    codes are reserved for entries that explicitly override the
    `FailureStatus`/`FailureCode` fields. Documenting the spec's
    extra response codes in the comment block keeps future readers
    from "fixing" the missing code mapping.

    in the comment block, e.g. "Future organization/* peers
    (API-0247 `*-all`, ...) should grep this entry first") was
    already used inside API-0099 → API-0101 and API-0098 → API-0100.

    cohesive when extending a roster, especially when an opener
    establishes a slug or fixture convention the rest of the tag
    will reuse.

    `jq '.paths."/organization.active" | {get_method:
    (.get.operationId), parameters: (.get.parameters // []),
    requestBody, responses: (.get.responses | keys)}'` against

## 2026-05-08 - API-0247 (organization-all)
- Covered Dokploy operation `organization-all` (GET /organization.all)
  by appending one `apiCoverageCase` entry to `coveredAPIOperations`
  in `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`. Second entry in the
  organization/* roster, completing the forward reference embedded
  in the API-0246 comment block (which named "API-0247 `*-all`" by
  story ID before the story landed).
- Spec source `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /organization.all >
  get`: `parameters: []`, no request body, responses 200/400/401/
  403/404/500. The 200 schema is `{}` with
  `additionalProperties: false` — byte-for-byte identical wire
  shape to API-0246 (`organization-active`). The semantic
  difference is scope: `*-all` returns every organization the
  bearer principal can see (membership-scoped list) vs. `*-active`
  returning the single currently-selected one. Both are
  parameter-free GETs from the harness's perspective, so
  SampleQuery/SamplePathParams/SampleBody stay unset and the
  canonical agent invocation is `yalla api call organization-all
  --input '{}' --json`.
- Representative-failure leg keeps the harness default of
  401→CodeAuth: auth is the universal failure mode every
  organization/* peer must re-prove. The spec's 404 exists for
  filter-shaped peers like the future API-0251 (`*-one`) rather
  than this list endpoint; story-specific FailureStatus/FailureCode
  overrides are reserved for entries where 404 is the canonical
  miss (id-shaped lookups).
- Comment block establishes the *intra-tag* twin GET precedent for
  organization/*: `*-active` (singular, principal-scoped) vs.
  `*-all` (plural, membership-scoped). This is analogous to the
  cross-tag pair API-0098 (`deployment-allCentralized`) and
  API-0100 (`deployment-queueList`) but inside one tag rather than
  across two operations sharing a path stem. Future organization/*
  peers introducing a third parameter-free GET (e.g. API-0248
  `*-allInvitations`) should grep API-0246 → API-0247 first to
  inherit the empty-fixture invariant.
- Files changed:
  - internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go (added API-0247 case)
  - ralph/prd.json (flipped passes:true for API-0247)
  - ralph/progress.txt (this entry)
- Verification run from repo root:
  - gofmt -w . — clean
  - go mod tidy — clean
  - go vet ./... — clean
  - go test ./... — PASS (internal/cli 1.203s)
  - go test -race ./... — PASS (internal/cli 2.529s)
- Optional tooling not invoked this iteration (golangci-lint,
  staticcheck, govulncheck, goreleaser, goimports): not installed
  locally, documented baseline since US-0001.
- Remaining risks / follow-ups: none for API-0247. The
  organization/* tag now has 2/10 covered (API-0246, API-0247).
  Eight peers remain (API-0248..0255). API-0248
  (`*-allInvitations`) is the next parameter-free GET and will
  inherit the empty-fixture pattern; API-0249 (`organization-
  create`) opens the tag's POST family and will introduce the
  `organization-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` body-fixture convention
  reserved by the API-0246 entry.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - Forward-reference completion across a single tag works the
    same way as across tag boundaries: API-0246's comment named
    "API-0247 `*-all`" by ID before that story landed; API-0247
    closes the loop by referencing API-0246 explicitly in its
    comment block. This makes the test file readable as a
    narrative and lets future readers grep a tag's first two
    entries to recover the harness conventions for that roster.
  - The "intra-tag twin GET" pattern (singular-scoped vs.
    plural-scoped with identical wire shape) is now distinct from
    the cross-tag twin precedent set by API-0098/API-0100.
    Documenting both in the API-0247 comment block gives future
    iterations two parallel precedents to grep when a tag
    exposes both a list endpoint and a principal-scoped lookup
    that share the empty-body 200 envelope.
  - Spec drift smoke test (re-confirmed for this story):
    `jq '.paths."/organization.all" | {get_method:
    (.get.operationId), parameters: (.get.parameters // []),
    responses: (.get.responses | keys)}'` against
    `internal/api/data/openapi.json` — empty parameters, no body,
    responses 200/400/401/403/404/500. Same one-liner used for
    API-0246, API-0100, and API-0101. Continuing to use this as
    the standard pre-comment validation step keeps spec-drift
    surprises out of the test file.

  endpoint, so registry/schema/manifest paths picked it up automatically the
  moment the coverage entry landed.

  subtests (registry / schema / manifest / success / failure) for
  `organization-allInvitations` and they all assert clean.

  organization/* peer (API-0249 `organization-create`) shifts the cohort

  another minimal-required-body POST. The forward-reference comment in

    (or simply omits the `parameters` key, as

    to *forward-reference the next sub-cohort* in the comment block. The
    API-0248 comment block flags that API-0249 leaves the parameter-free
    GET trio behind, so the next contributor lands on a precedent that
    actually matches the new shape (required-body POST) instead of
    mechanically copy-pasting an empty-fixture entry that would skip the
    body-forwarding wire assertion.
  - `runAPICoverageSuccess` already asserts `Authorization: Bearer …` and
    empty query string for parameter-free GETs, so adding a third copy of
    the same SuccessResponse: `{}` literal is genuinely zero-risk —
    `go test -race ./...` confirmed no shared-state regressions across
    the three concurrent sub-table entries.

    API-XXXX story for an existing tag needs to touch when the operation
    fits the default success/failure shape. The harness
    (`runAPICoverageSuccess` and the registry/schema invariants tests)
    handles all four acceptance criteria — registry presence, raw API
    call, schema get, and manifest coverage — automatically, so a new
    entry alone satisfies every line item without per-story Go code.

    organization/* tag mean `SuccessResponse: ` + literal `{}` is the
    correct fixture; do not invent payload fields, the schema rejects
    them and the success-leg assertion does not exercise projection on
    these operations.

    literal pattern: a JSON object with the required field set to the
    deterministic-but-clearly-fake token `yalla-coverage-<short>-<storyID>`.
    The token must include the four-digit story ID so `git grep "0250"`
    finds the fixture from any reviewer's terminal.
  - The fixture-comment header convention (every-optional-populated,
    per-case fixture token, default 401 failure leg) carries forward from
    API-0249's future-contributors block; succeeding entries should grep
    API-0249 first for the design rationale and only re-state per-entry
    deltas (here: closed-shape single-required-scalar instead of
    `{name, logo}`).

    manifest inclusion, httptest success, representative-failure leg) for
    a `single-required-scalar` POST — appending one struct literal
    completes the story without any new test plumbing.
  - `organization-removeInvitation` notably **omits** the 404 response code
    that the lookup-shape GET `organization-one` declares — Dokploy's tRPC
    surface collapses missing-invitation into a 400 (validation) rather
    than a 404 (not found). The harness default 401→CodeAuth failure leg
    is the right call; do not invent a per-case 404 representative-failure
    variant for delete-by-id mutations in this tag.
  - Per-case fixture token convention reaffirmed:
    `<tag>-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` → `yalla-coverage-org-removeInvitation-0252`
    keeps `git grep` traceable without colliding with API-0249/0250
    fixtures (`yalla-coverage-org-create-0249`,
    `yalla-coverage-org-delete-0250`).
---

## 2026-05-08 - API-0254 - Cover organization-update (POST /organization.update)

- Added a new `apiCoverageCase` entry for `organization-update` to the
  shared `coveredAPIOperations` table in
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`. The entry mirrors the API-0253
  `organization-setDefault` literal but breaks out of the

  fields (`organizationId`, `name`) plus the optional `logo` (which the
  fixture intentionally omits to keep the closed-shape contract
  focused on the minimum-required body shape — same posture as
  API-0249 `organization-create` with its own optional fields).
- Fixture tokens follow the established
  `<tag>-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` deterministic-but-clearly-fake pattern
  with `-id` / `-name` suffixes to disambiguate the two required
  scalars within a single body without losing the story-ID trail.
- Files changed: `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`,
  `ralph/prd.json` (passes: true for API-0254), `ralph/progress.txt`.

  `go test ./...`, `go test -race ./...` — all green.

  raw-call success, and representative-failure (401 → CodeAuth)

  per-story scaffolding required.
- Remaining risks: none specific to this story. The `organization` tag
  has one mutation peer left (API-0255 `*-updateMemberRole`) before
  the tag closes.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The `apiCoverageCase` table is the single source of truth for
    every `api-operation` PRD story — adding an entry automatically
    drives manifest, registry, schema, call-success, and
    representative-failure tests via the harness. There is no need
    to scaffold per-story files.
  - When the spec declares optional body fields (e.g. `logo` here,
    `slug` in `organization-create`), the established posture is to
    omit them from the fixture so the success-leg envelope assertion
    stays focused on `data.method` / `data.status` rather than
    drifting into payload projection.
  - `<tag>-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` fixture tokens with `-id` / `-name`
    suffixes are the right way to disambiguate multiple required
    scalars within a single body without losing the `git grep` trail
    back to the PRD story.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0255
- Added `coveredAPIOperations` entry for `organization-updateMemberRole`
  (POST /organization.updateMemberRole, tag `organization`) — the sixth
  and final mutation in the organization/* roster, closing the tag arc
  opened by API-0246.
- Used the `{required-scalar, required-scalar}` body shape forecast by
  the API-0254 future-contributors block: required `memberId` (free-form
  string) and required `role` (enum-pinned to `"admin"` so the
  enum-constrained branch is exercised; the alternate `"member"` value
  is intentionally not duplicated). No optional fields exist in the
  spec, keeping the closed-shape contract strict.
- Files changed:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended API-0255 case
  - `ralph/prd.json` — flipped API-0255 `passes` to `true`
  - `ralph/progress.txt` — this entry
- Verification run from repo root:
  - `gofmt -w .` ✓
  - `go mod tidy` ✓ (no changes)
  - `go vet ./...` ✓
  - `go test ./... -count=1` ✓ all packages pass
  - `go test -race ./... -count=1` ✓ all packages pass
  - `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`, and
    `goreleaser` not installed locally; documented in earlier entries
    as the verification-tools baseline gap.
- Remaining risks / follow-ups:
  - Per the API-0249 design-rationale header, this entry **closes the
    organization/* tag**. The next tag in the priority-2 roster
    (project/*, API-0290 onward) opens a fresh arc — a future
    contributor picking up that arc should re-establish a new
    design-rationale header rather than back-reference the
    organization/* literals here, because the
    `<tag>-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` namespace is strictly per-tag and
    cross-tag template sharing has historically produced false-positive
    `git grep` collisions when slug names overlap (e.g. a future
    `*-update*` peer in another tag).
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The two-required-scalars body shape (`{required-scalar,
    required-scalar}` with zero optionals) is now established as a
    sibling pattern to the `{single-required-scalar}` arc. Future
    POSTs with two-or-more required scalars and zero optionals can
    copy the API-0255 literal verbatim with field names swapped.
  - Enum-constrained scalars (here: `role` ∈ `{admin, member}`) should
    be pinned to a fixed enum value rather than receiving the
    `<tag>-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` token, because injecting a unique-
    but-invalid string would defeat the schema-validator coverage
    the fixture is designed to support. Other free-form scalars in
    the same body still get the deterministic-but-clearly-fake token.
  - The harness "default failure leg = 401 → CodeAuth" remains the
    right reductionist posture for state-mutation peers; 400
    (validation) is exercised once at the package level by API-0001
    and does not need to be re-asserted on every minimum-required-body
    POST in the roster.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0292 - Cover project-create (POST /project.create)

- Added the `apiCoverageCase` entry for `project-create` to
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`, sorted immediately after API-0291.
  This is the first project/* mutation peer and the first project/* entry
  to populate `SampleBody`, opening the `proj-cov-*` slug namespace
  reserved by API-0290's design-rationale header.
- Spec-derived body shape (verified against
  `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /project.create > post`):
  `name` is the only required field; `description` (anyOf [string, null])
  and `env` (string) are optional. Per the API-0010 / API-0014 / API-0249
  every-optional-populated convention re-affirmed across the ai/*,
  application/*, and organization/* mutation rosters, all three fields
  are populated with deterministic-but-clearly-fake values so the wire
  payload exercises the full envelope. Per-case fixture token
  `proj-cov-create-0292` is unique across the project/* roster and never
  collides with the `org-cov-*` namespace.

  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` (new struct literal, +93 lines)
  - `ralph/prd.json` (passes flag flipped to true for API-0292)
  - `ralph/progress.txt` (this entry)

  - `go mod tidy` — no changes
  - `go vet ./...` — clean
  - `go test ./... -count=1` — PASS (every package)
  - `go test -race ./... -count=1` — PASS (every package)
  - Per-story confirmation: all five subtests
    (`Registry`, `Schema`, `Manifest`, `SuccessRoundTrip`,
    `RepresentativeFailure`) PASS for `API-0292/project-create`.
  - `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`, and
    `goreleaser` not installed locally; documented in earlier entries
    as the verification-tools baseline gap.
- Results: harness loop now runs all five contract checks for API-0292
  alongside the prior 100 covered operations; no source files outside
  the `apiCoverageCase` table needed editing — registry, schema, manifest,
  success exchange, and representative-failure paths are all data-driven
  off `coveredAPIOperations`.
- Remaining risks / follow-ups:
  - API-0293 `project-duplicate` is the next mutation in the project/*
    arc. The future-contributors block in this entry forecasts it as a
    likely two-required-scalar POST (`projectId`, `name`) that can copy
    the API-0255 `organization-updateMemberRole` shape verbatim with
    field names swapped — but the SampleBody literal must always be
    cross-checked against `data/openapi.json` before assuming the
    forecast holds.
  - API-0294 `project-one` will be the first filter-shaped peer in the
    arc and per the API-0290 / API-0291 reservation is the legitimate
    home for a 404 → CodeNotFound representative-failure leg.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - When opening a tag's mutation cohort (here: project/* mutation arc
    after two parameter-free GETs), the first mutation entry is also
    the first opportunity to *anchor* the per-tag fixture-token
    namespace declared by the tag's opening parameter-free GET. The
    design-rationale comment block must therefore re-state the
    namespace rule from the opener (`proj-cov-*` here, not `org-cov-*`)
    so future contributors do not accidentally cross the tag boundary.
  - Optional-field surfaces with `anyOf [string, null]` (here:
    `description`) are still safe to populate with a plain string in the
    fixture — the `null` alternative is exercised at the schema-validator
    level by API-0001's package-level negative coverage rather than per
    operation. Populating the string branch keeps the success-leg
    JSON forwarding assertion non-trivial without sacrificing the
    every-optional-populated invariant.
  - The `env` field in `project-create` is a free-form string (not a
    map or list of `KEY=VALUE`), so a multi-line literal with embedded
    `\n` is the most informative fixture value — it exercises the
    request-body byte forwarding path through quoted JSON without
    drifting into payload projection that the success-leg assertion
    intentionally avoids.
  - For the first mutation peer in a fresh tag arc, the mechanical
    work is still a single struct literal append. No code under
    `internal/api/`, `internal/cli/api_call*.go`, `internal/cli/schema*.go`,
    or `internal/cli/manifest*.go` needs editing — the operation
    registry, schema generator, and manifest renderer are spec-driven
    from `internal/api/data/openapi.json`.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0293
- Added apiCoverageCase entry for `project-duplicate`
  (POST /project.duplicate, tag `project`) to
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`, sorted immediately after
  API-0292. This is the second mutation peer in the project/*
  roster and the first project/* entry to exercise a nested
  array-of-objects body shape.
- Spec-derived body shape (verified against
  `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /project.duplicate > post`):
  required scalars `sourceEnvironmentId` + `name`; optional plain
  string `description`; optional booleans `includeServices`
  (default `true`) and `duplicateInSameProject` (default `false`);
  optional array-of-objects `selectedServices` with each element
  carrying required `id` (string) and required `type` enum
  (application|postgres|mariadb|mongo|mysql|redis|compose). Per
  the API-0010 / API-0014 / API-0249 / API-0292 every-optional-
  populated convention all four optionals are populated. Booleans
  are flipped to the *inverse* of their spec defaults so the
  success-leg byte forwarding actually transmits the chosen
  value rather than coinciding with what a server-side default
  would emit. `selectedServices` carries one element with
  `type: "application"` enum-pinned (per the API-0255 enum-
  constrained-scalar rule). Per-case fixture token base
  `proj-cov-duplicate-0293`; nested element id
  `app-cov-duplicate-0293-svc1`; both are unique across the
  project/* roster and never collide with `org-cov-*`.
- Files changed:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` (new struct literal,
    +118 lines including comment header)
  - `ralph/prd.json` (passes flag flipped to true for API-0293)
  - `ralph/progress.txt` (this entry)
- Verification (repo root):
  - `gofmt -w .` — clean
  - `go mod tidy` — no changes
  - `go vet ./...` — clean
  - `go test ./... -count=1` — all packages OK
  - `go test -race ./... -count=1` — all packages OK
  - `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`,
    and `goreleaser` not installed locally; documented in earlier
    entries as the verification-tools baseline gap.
- Results: harness loop now runs the five contract checks
  (registry / schema / manifest / success round-trip /
  representative failure) for API-0293 alongside the prior 102
  covered operations. No source files outside the
  `apiCoverageCase` table needed editing — the operation
  registry, schema generator, and manifest renderer are spec-
  driven from `internal/api/data/openapi.json`.
- Remaining risks / follow-ups:
  - API-0294 `project-one` is the next entry in the project/*
    arc and per the API-0290 / API-0291 / API-0292 reservation
    is the legitimate home for the project/* tag's first
    filter-shaped GET. It will be the first project/* peer to
    populate `SampleQuery` (the spec declares a required
    `projectId` query parameter and no body), and the first
    appropriate place in the project/* roster to lift the
    harness default failure leg from 401 → CodeAuth to
    404 → CodeNotFound — auth (401) has been re-proven across
    every project/* peer so far, and 404 is precisely the
    failure shape a single-resource lookup must surface.
  - API-0295 `project-remove`, API-0297 `project-update` are
    expected to share the project-duplicate `{required, +optional}`
    pattern but with simpler bodies (likely
    `{projectId}` for remove and `{projectId, name?, description?}`
    for update). They can copy this entry's slug-namespace
    discipline but must rederive their `SampleBody` literal from
    the spec — the array-of-objects shape used here is
    project-duplicate-specific and does not transfer.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - For mutation peers with optional booleans that have non-
    `false` spec defaults, **flip the fixture value to the
    inverse of the default** (`includeServices: true` (default)
    → `false` (fixture); `duplicateInSameProject: false`
    (default) → `true` (fixture)). This keeps the success-leg
    body forwarding invariant observable end-to-end: a server
    rendering server-side defaults would round-trip the same
    bytes regardless of CLI behaviour, but inverted booleans
    force the wire to actually carry the chosen value through
    the CLI → API client → httptest server path. The
    every-optional-populated convention is therefore really
    every-optional-populated-with-non-default-where-feasible.
  - Nested array-of-objects body fixtures pre-exist in the ai/*
    roster (API-0004 `ai-deploy` with `domains[]` and
    `configFiles[]`) — when adding the first such fixture in a
    new tag arc, grep for the prior tag's array-of-objects entry
    to inherit the indentation style (`[` / `\n\t\t\t\t{` /
    `\n\t\t\t}` / `\n\t\t]`) rather than re-deriving it.
  - Enum-constrained array element fields follow the same rule
    as scalar enums established at API-0255 `organization-
    updateMemberRole`: pin to **one** fixed enum value and do not
    duplicate alternates across additional array elements. The
    schema-validator coverage for the alternate enum branches is
    handled at the spec-level by the registry, not at the per-
    operation success-leg fixture.
  - `description` in the project/* tag is not uniformly nullable:
    API-0292 `project-create` declared `anyOf [string, null]`,
    while API-0293 `project-duplicate` declares plain `string`.
    Always cross-check the per-operation spec for nullability —
    the every-optional-populated rule's "populate the string
    branch" guidance from API-0292 still works for both cases
    (the populated string is valid in either schema), but
    schema-level assertions about which branch is exercised
    differ between the two peers.
---

- Closed the project/* mutation arc (API-0292 `*-create`, API-0293

  eight-peer project/* tag (filter peers API-0290 / API-0291 /
  API-0294 / API-0296). The body shape is `{required projectId,
  optional name, optional description (anyOf [string, null]),
  optional createdAt, optional organizationId, optional env}` —
  the largest scalar-only mutation body in the project/* roster.
- Files changed:
  - internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go (appended apiCoverageCase
    entry for API-0297 with the every-optional-populated SampleBody
    `{projectId, name, description, createdAt, organizationId,
    env}`; inserted after the API-0296 project-search block to keep
    StoryID-sorted order; design-rationale header documents the
    project/* arc-closing convention, the nullable `description`
    treatment matching API-0292 `project-create`, and the
    RFC 3339 `createdAt` literal that survives a future tightening
    of the schema to `format: date-time`.)
  - ralph/prd.json (passes:true for API-0297)
- Verification (repo root):
  - gofmt -w .
  - go mod tidy (no diff)
  - go vet ./...
  - go test ./... -count=1
  - go test -race ./... -count=1
- Results: all green (no diffs, no failures, no races).
- Remaining risks or follow-ups: none — API-0297 closes the project/*
  arc and is gated by the same five-part harness that gates the
  prior 107 API-XXXX stories (registry presence, schema reachability,
  manifest inclusion, httptest success leg, representative failure
  leg).
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The `*-update` shape across the Dokploy spec is consistently
    `{required <resource>Id, optional mutable fields...}` — the
    project/* peer is the canonical template for future tag arcs
    when their `*-update` peer comes up. Future contributors should
    grep this entry first for the required-discriminator-plus-
    every-optional-populated pattern, then layer the per-tag mutable
    fields on top.
  - The nullable `description` (`anyOf [string, null]`) recurs
    across project/* peers (`project-create`, `project-update`) but
    NOT in `project-duplicate` (plain string). When populating, use
    a deterministic-fake string rather than `null` — the populated-
    string fixture exercises the wire-forwarding invariant more
    strongly than `null` would, and a future schema validator that
    rejects `null` (e.g. one that drops the `null` branch from the
    `anyOf`) will still accept the fixture.
  - `createdAt` is typed as a plain `string` in the spec but the
    fixture uses an RFC 3339 UTC literal so the wire fixture
    survives a future schema tightening to `format: date-time` and
    stays human-readable. Same convention should apply to any
    future timestamp-shaped scalar typed loosely as `string`.
  - With the project/* arc closed, the next priority story is
    API-0335 `server-all` (a P2 GET, opening the server/* tag).
    Server-tag peers should grep this project/* arc for the per-tag
    fixture-isolation rule (open with `srv-cov-*` or similar slug
    namespace, never back-reference `proj-cov-*`).

  server/* roster) still have `passes: false` and should follow the
  fixture-isolation header established here.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The `coveredAPIOperations` slice in `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`
    is indented with **one tab per entry** (`\t{ ... \t},`) and **two tabs
    per field**. Field indentation does NOT match the visual indent the
    Read tool surfaces when line-number prefixes are present — when adding
    an entry, anchor on a unique inner line (e.g. `// projection.\n
    \t\tSuccessResponse: \`{}\`,\n\t},\n}` for the slice tail) and confirm
    raw bytes via `od -c` if Edit's "string not found" error fires.
  - For parameter-free GETs whose 200 schema is `{}`, the entry is purely
    `StoryID/OperationID/Method/Path/Tag/SuccessResponse: \`{}\`` — leaving
    `SampleQuery`/`SamplePathParams`/`SampleBody` unset is the documented
    "empty fixture" invariant inherited from API-0006 `ai-getAll` and
    API-0246 `organization-active`. The default failure leg
    (401 -> CodeAuth) is the universal auth re-prove and overrides the
    spec's 404 declaration; per-tag-opener convention reserves
    404 -> CodeNotFound for filter-shaped peers like the upcoming
    `server-one` (API-0342).
  - PRD edits with `python3 -c json.dump` are safe because the PRD file is
    canonical UTF-8 JSON; the standard library preserves Unicode without
    re-escaping (which would alter the file's public-API string content).

  mutation arc; future server/* mutation peers (e.g. a hypothetical
  `server-update`) should grep this entry first for the
  `srv-cov-create-0338` fixture-token namespace pattern and the
  `serverType: "deploy"` enum-literal pinning convention. Future
  `*-create` peers in other tags should grep this entry first for the
  all-required-fields-with-anyOf-null nullable plus enum-constrained
  scalar shape.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The `apiCoverageCase` harness in `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`
    is the canonical sign-off for every API-XXXX story. Adding coverage
    for a new operation is a one-line struct literal append — the
    harness handles registry presence, schema reachability, manifest
    inclusion, the httptest success exchange, and the representative
    failure path automatically. **Never duplicate test boilerplate.**
  - `anyOf [string, null]` schemas are populated with strings (NOT
    `null` literals) per the populated-branch convention shared with
    API-0292 `project-create` and API-0297 `project-update` — a
    populated value proves the wire-forwarding invariant more strongly
    than a `null` would.
  - Closed-enum scalars (e.g. `serverType: ["deploy", "build"]`) MUST
    be pinned to a declared enum member in the SampleBody so a future
    schema validator that tightens the enum will not reject the
    fixture. The choice of literal is arbitrary but should be
    deterministic and reused across same-tag peers for diff-friendliness.
  - The per-tag fixture-isolation rule (established at API-0246
    `organization-active`, reasserted at API-0290 `project-all`, and
    again at API-0335 `server-all`) requires every fixture token in a
    given tag to share a `<tag-prefix>-cov-*` namespace and **must not**
    back-reference a different tag's namespace literals.
  - The 404 → CodeNotFound representative-failure code is reserved for
    filter-shaped by-id peers (e.g. `*-one`) within a tag, NOT for
    create/update mutations — those keep the harness default 401 →
    CodeAuth because auth is the universal failure mode.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0340
- What was implemented: Coverage for `server-getServerMetrics` (GET
  /server.getServerMetrics, tag `server`, three required query
  parameters `url` / `token` / `dataPoints`, all string per the spec).
  Added a single `apiCoverageCase` struct literal to
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` with a `SampleQuery` that
  populates all three keys; the existing harness derives registry
  invariants, schema reachability, manifest inclusion, the
  httptest-backed success exchange (per-key `r.URL.Query()`
  re-assertion), and the default 401 → CodeAuth failure leg from
  that single literal.
- Files changed:
  * `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` (new struct literal)
  * `ralph/prd.json` (flipped `API-0340.passes` to `true`)
  * `ralph/progress.txt` (this entry)
- Verification run:
  * `gofmt -w .` → clean
  * `goimports` → not installed (consistent with prior iterations)
  * `go mod tidy` → no changes
  * `go vet ./...` → exit 0
  * `go test ./...` → all 9 packages PASS
  * `go test -race ./...` → all 9 packages PASS
  * `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`, `goreleaser` →
    not installed (documented in prior progress entries; no change
    this iteration)
- Results: 114/462 PRD stories now passing; CI-equivalent local
  checks all green.
- Remaining risks or follow-ups: None specific to this story. The
  multi-query-param shape is now first-classed in the coverage
  harness; future `*-getServerMetrics`-style peers in other tags
  and any other multi-required-query GET should mirror this entry.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **API-0340 was the FIRST multi-key SampleQuery case in the
    entire coverage roster.** I ran a regex sweep before adding
    the entry — every prior `SampleQuery` block (24 cases across
    ai/*, organization/*, project/*, and the API-0339
    `server-getDefaultCommand` opener) declared exactly one key.
    The harness already supports multi-key maps natively
    (`for k, want := range tc.SampleQuery { … seenQuery[k] … }`)
    so no harness change was needed — but document the milestone
    in the case comment so future grep-driven discovery works.
  - **Per-key fixture token slug pattern.** When a single case
    has multiple secrets/values, prefix every value with the
    same `<tag>-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` base so a leaked value is
    traceable to one story. I used:
      * `url`        → `https://srv-cov-getServerMetrics-0340.example.test`
      * `token`      → `srv-cov-getServerMetrics-0340-token`
      * `dataPoints` → `50`
    The `.example.test` reserved domain (RFC 2606) means even an
    accidental real-world fetch can never escape the test sandbox.
  - **`token` query param is NOT a Dokploy auth credential.** It
    is a *target-server* token that Dokploy forwards downstream to
    a remote agent host — orthogonal to the redaction security
    suite's bearer-token sentinel checks. Picking a fixture-shaped
    value (no `Bearer` prefix, no base64-looking entropy) keeps
    the security suite clean. The success envelope's `URL` field
    will be redacted by `output.RedactURL` per the existing
    `redaction_security_test.go::?token=` test, but the harness
    does not assert on the `URL` field — only on
    `OperationID`/`Method`/`Status`/`SchemaVersion` — so no
    interaction.
  - **`schemaType: string` for numeric values.** The spec declares
    `dataPoints` as `string` even though it is conceptually a
    count; the `--input` JSON's `query` field is `map[string][]string`,
    so the value goes in as `"50"` not `50`. Mirroring the spec
    type verbatim is the safe default for any future query param
    that "looks numeric but is declared string".
  - **API-0339 commentary forward-referenced API-0340.** The
    `server-getDefaultCommand` case explicitly named
    `server-getServerMetrics` as "API-0340 with three query params"
    in its forward-peer list. Future iterations should keep this
    forward-reference convention so each new case both grounds
    itself in priors and seeds the next one.

  checks all green.

  next server/* peer (API-0342 `server-one`) introduces the by-id
  query shape AND the 404 → CodeNotFound failure-leg override; the
  API-0341 entry's forward-reference comment should orient that
  iteration. Other parameter-free server/* peers in the queue
  (API-0343 `server-publicIp`, API-0345 `server-security`,
  API-0349 `server-validate`, API-0350 `server-withSSHKey`) should
  mirror this entry verbatim.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **First parameter-free server/* GET to follow query-shaped
    peers.** API-0335/0336/0337 were a contiguous parameter-free
    GET cohort; API-0338 then introduced a body, API-0339 a
    single query param, and API-0340 three query params. API-0341
    re-enters the parameter-free shape *after* the SampleQuery
    block, so the per-case literal must explicitly re-omit
    SampleQuery / SamplePathParams / SampleBody. The harness's
    `r.URL.RawQuery == ""` assertion in `runAPICoverageSuccess`
    catches any accidental copy-paste that leaks the API-0340
    query keys (the `seenQuery` map is per-test, not slice-wide,
    so this risk is purely a literal-typo class — but worth
    naming explicitly because the test would still pass on
    everything *except* the one fresh case).
  - **Empty-object `{}` 200 schema is a Dokploy-wide convention,
    not a parameter-free quirk.** Even semantically rich
    operations like `*-getServerTime` (which would conventionally
    return a scalar timestamp) and `*-count` (which would
    conventionally return a scalar count) declare `{}` with
    `additionalProperties: false`. The pattern of "exercise the
    documented contract, not a speculative future tightening" was
    first noted at API-0337 `server-count` and now reasserted
    here; future `*-getX`-style endpoints should grep both
    entries for the rationale.
  - **Forward-reference comments earn their keep at this point.**
    API-0335's comment block already named API-0341
    `server-getServerTime` in its forward-peer list, which made
    discovery a single grep. Continuing to seed forward
    references (this entry now names API-0342 `server-one` for
    its 404 → CodeNotFound override and API-0343/0345/0349/0350
    for parameter-free GET peers) keeps the chain navigable from
    either direction. Future iterations should preserve this.
  - **Adding a parameter-free coverage entry remains a pure-data
    change** — no production code, registry, schema, or manifest
    edits are needed because the embedded OpenAPI document
    already advertises the operation. The four parameterised
    tests (`TestAPICoverage_RegistryInvariants`,
    `TestAPICoverage_SchemaCommandWorks`,
    `TestAPICoverage_ManifestIncludes…`,
    `TestAPICoverage_SuccessExchange` plus the failure-leg
    parameterised test) extend automatically. This was true for
    every prior parameter-free entry and remains true here.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0342
- What was implemented: Coverage for `server-one` (GET /server.one,
  tag `server`, single required query parameter `serverId` of type
  string). Added a single `apiCoverageCase` struct literal to
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` immediately after the API-0341
  `server-getServerTime` case. **First entry in the entire 116-case
  roster to override the failure leg to 404 → CodeNotFound** — the
  reservation that the API-0290 `project-all` design header opened
  and that API-0294 `project-one` deferred to the next available
  `*-one` peer. The harness already supports the override natively
  (`runAPICoverageFailure` reads `tc.FailureStatus` / `tc.FailureCode`
  with sensible defaults), so no harness change was needed.
- Files changed:
  * `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` (one new struct literal,
    ~120 lines including design rationale; sets `FailureStatus:
    http.StatusNotFound` and `FailureCode: yerr.CodeNotFound`)
  * `ralph/prd.json` (flipped `API-0342.passes` to `true`)
  * `ralph/progress.txt` (this entry)
- Verification run:
  * `gofmt -w .` → clean
  * `goimports` → not installed (consistent with prior iterations)
  * `go mod tidy` → no changes
  * `go vet ./...` → exit 0
  * `go test ./...` → all 9 packages PASS
  * `go test -race ./...` → all 9 packages PASS
  * `go test ./internal/cli -run TestAPICoverage -count=1 -v` →
    every subtest PASS, including the new
    `TestAPICoverage_RepresentativeFailure/API-0342/server-one`
    which exercises the 404 → CodeNotFound override end-to-end
  * `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`, `goreleaser` →
    not installed (documented in prior progress entries; no change
    this iteration)
- Results: 116/462 PRD stories now passing; CI-equivalent local
  checks all green.
- Remaining risks or follow-ups: None specific to this story. The
  next server/* peer (API-0343 `server-publicIp`) returns to the
  parameter-free GET shape established at API-0335..API-0337 /
  API-0341 and should keep the harness default 401 → CodeAuth.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **Activating a reserved harness override is a pure-data
    change.** API-0290's design header reserved 404 → CodeNotFound
    for the canonical `*-one` peer; API-0294 `project-one` chose
    not to act on it for tag-roster symmetry. API-0342
    `server-one` is the first peer to *act*, simply by setting
    `FailureStatus: http.StatusNotFound, FailureCode:
    yerr.CodeNotFound` in the struct literal — no harness or
    runner edit required. The two zero-default fields exist
    precisely for this kind of opt-in.
  - **`tc.FailureCode` must use the typed `yerr.Code` constant**
    (e.g. `yerr.CodeNotFound`), NOT a string literal. The struct
    field is `yerr.Code` (a defined string type) and the comparison
    in `runAPICoverageFailure` does `string(failCode)` for the
    stderr substring match. Using `"E_NOT_FOUND"` would compile
    (Go allows untyped string→named-string conversion in struct
    literals) but breaks the contract — always import via the
    `yerr "github.com/JuribaDev/yalla/internal/errors"` alias
    already in scope and reference the constant.
  - **The 404 override semantically belongs on the by-id peer of
    the tag whose call site most often encounters cold-cache
    fetches**, not necessarily the *first* `*-one` peer in the
    roster. project-one (API-0294) would have been the alphabetic
    first opportunity, but the project/* arc punted to keep its
    arc symmetric. server-one is a stronger fit because Dokploy
    server records are pinned to UUIDs an agent will frequently
    refetch on cold-cache restart, where 404 (record deleted
    between cache fill and fetch) is a more common runtime
    failure than 401 (process already proved auth). Future
    contributors picking the next "primary failure mode is
    not-found" peer should grep this entry's design rationale
    block first.
  - **Cross-tag `*-one` shape is now extensively documented.**
    By the time API-0342 lands, the by-id GET shape has been
    exercised at API-0008 `ai-one`, API-0021 `application-one`,
    API-0084 `compose-one`, API-0251 `organization-one`, and
    API-0294 `project-one`. Each shares the byte-for-byte wire
    contract (single required scalar query param, empty-object
    200 response, 401/404 in the failure surface). Future tags'
    `*-one` peers are pure copy-and-rename — only the ID field
    name and the per-tag fixture slug change.
  - **Forward-reference comments paid off here.** API-0335's
    opener block, plus API-0336/0337/0341's commentary, all
    explicitly named API-0342 `server-one` as the canonical home
    for the 404 override, which made the design decision a
    single grep instead of a re-derivation. The chain continues:
    this entry now names API-0343 `server-publicIp` for the
    parameter-free re-anchoring.
---

  invariants, schema command, manifest inclusion, httptest-backed

  The next server/* parameter-free GET peers in the queue
  (API-0345 `server-security`, API-0349 `server-validate`,
  API-0350 `server-withSSHKey`) should mirror this entry verbatim
  — pure copy-and-rename, only StoryID / OperationID / Path
  change. The next case in the server/* roster, API-0344
  `server-remove`, is a POST mutation that re-introduces the
  body-mutation shape last exercised at API-0338 `server-create`
  and will need a fixture body following the
  `srv-cov-remove-0344` slug; the API-0343 entry's
  forward-reference comment names it explicitly.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **Re-anchoring after a one-time failure-leg override.**
    API-0342 `server-one` introduced the 404 → CodeNotFound
    override that was reserved at API-0290 / API-0294 / API-0335.
    API-0343 is the **first case after that override** to revert
    to the default 401 → CodeAuth, and the per-case literal must
    **explicitly omit** the FailureStatus / FailureCode lines —
    they are not inherited across slice elements (the harness
    fall-through is `tc.FailureStatus == 0` → 401, `tc.FailureCode
    == ""` → CodeAuth) but a copy-paste from API-0342 would
    silently flip the assertion to 404. The new entry's
    "Failure-leg fields are intentionally omitted" comment block
    documents this trap for the next contributor; future peers
    that re-anchor after any override should preserve it.
  - **Second parameter-free server/* peer to follow query-shaped
    peers.** API-0341 was the first (after API-0339/0340);
    API-0343 is the second (after API-0342's by-id `serverId`
    query). The harness's `r.URL.RawQuery == ""` assertion in
    `runAPICoverageSuccess` catches any accidental copy-paste
    that leaks the API-0342 `serverId` key, but the
    intent-documenting comment block (re-asserted from API-0341)
    is the cheaper safety net — future contributors who notice
    they are about to copy a SampleQuery line should grep this
    entry's commentary first.
  - **Forward-reference chain remains the cheapest design tool.**
    API-0341 named API-0343 as the next parameter-free re-anchor;
    API-0342 named API-0343 as the next case to "return to the
    parameter-free GET shape" with the default 401 leg restored.
    Both forward references made the design decision a single
    `git grep "API-0343"` instead of a re-derivation. The chain
    continues: this entry now names API-0344 `server-remove` as
    the next body-mutation re-introduction, and API-0345 / 0349 /
    0350 as the remaining parameter-free peers.
  - **Adding a parameter-free coverage entry remains a pure-data
    change** — no production code, registry, schema, or manifest
    edits are needed because the embedded OpenAPI document
    already advertises the operation. This was true for every
    prior parameter-free entry and remains true here. The
    parameterised tests pick up the new case automatically.

  `TestAPICoverage_RepresentativeFailure`) extend automatically.
  This is the **second body-mutation peer** in the server/* tag
  (after API-0338 `server-create`) and the **smallest possible
  body** in the entire server/* roster — a single required
  string scalar, no nullables/enums/nested shapes. Re-introduces
  the body-mutation arc after the parameter-free GET re-anchor
  at API-0343; the API-0343 forward-reference comment named the
  `srv-cov-remove-0344` slug explicitly.

  - `ralph/prd.json` (API-0344 `passes` → true)

  * `goimports` → not installed (consistent with prior iterations)
  * `go mod tidy` → no changes
  * `go vet ./...` → exit 0
  * `go test ./...` → all 9 packages PASS
  * `go test -race ./...` → all 9 packages PASS

    (every parameterised subtest including
    `TestAPICoverage_SuccessRoundTrip/API-0344/server-remove`
    and `TestAPICoverage_RepresentativeFailure/API-0344/server-remove`)
  * `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`, `goreleaser` →
    not installed (documented in prior progress entries; no
    change this iteration)
- Results: 118/462 PRD stories now passing; CI-equivalent local
  checks all green.
- Remaining risks or follow-ups: None specific to this story.
  The next case in the server/* roster, API-0345
  `server-security`, returns to the parameter-free GET shape (a
  sixth member of that sub-cohort, after API-0335..API-0337,
  API-0341, and API-0343) and should mirror API-0343 verbatim
  — pure copy-and-rename, only StoryID / OperationID / Path
  change. Subsequent server/* mutation peers (API-0346
  `server-setup`, API-0347 `server-setupMonitoring`, API-0348
  `server-update`) should grep this entry first for the minimal
  body-mutation shape; the API-0344 entry's forward-reference
  comment names them all explicitly.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **Minimal-body POST is the smallest mutation shape Dokploy
    declares.** API-0338 `server-create` carried seven required
    fields including a nullable `description` and an
    enum-constrained `serverType`; API-0344 `server-remove`
    carries exactly one — a required `serverId` string scalar.
    This makes API-0344 the canonical reference for future
    "single-id POST removal" mutations across other tags
    (`*-remove`, `*-delete`, `*-archive` style endpoints), which
    is a recurring Dokploy idiom. Future contributors picking
    up a `*-remove` peer in any tag should grep this entry first
    before deriving the SampleBody shape, and should keep the
    SampleBody to the documented required field set rather than
    speculatively adding optional metadata.
  - **No-404-declared body mutations keep the harness default
    even when semantically by-id.** `server-remove` semantically
    operates on a serverId and could plausibly 404 on a missing
    record at runtime, but the embedded spec does not enumerate
    404 in its response set (200/400/401/403/500 only). The
    "exercise the documented contract, not a speculative future
    tightening" rule (reasserted at API-0337 `server-count`,
    API-0341 `server-getServerTime`, and API-0343
    `server-publicIp` for response *payload* shapes) applies
    equally to *failure* leg shapes: the API-0342 `server-one`
    404 → CodeNotFound override is reserved for endpoints that
    actually declare 404 in their spec, and pre-emptively
    overriding here would diverge from the embedded contract.
    Future `*-remove` peers should grep this paragraph before
    copying API-0342's failure-leg lines.
  - **Cross-shape diff-friendliness via shared slug roots.**
    The server/* roster now spans five shape families
    (parameter-free GET, single-required-query GET,
    multi-required-query GET, by-id GET with 404 override, and
    body mutation in two body-size variants). All ten cases
    share the `srv-cov-*` slug root with a per-case suffix
    encoding the operation slug + StoryID
    (`srv-cov-create-0338`, `srv-cov-getServerMetrics-0340`,
    `srv-cov-one-0342`, `srv-cov-remove-0344`, etc.). This makes
    a `git grep srv-cov-` instantly reveal the entire server/*
    fixture inventory and any accidental cross-tag leaks.
    Future tags should preserve this convention.
  - **Forward-reference chain remains the navigation backbone.**
    API-0343 named API-0344 as the "next body-mutation
    re-introduction"; this entry now names API-0345 as the next
    parameter-free GET re-anchor and API-0346/0347/0348 as the
    upcoming server/* mutation peers. The chain continues to
    make per-case design decisions a single `git grep "API-XXXX"`
    rather than a re-derivation. Future iterations should
    preserve this pattern — each new case should both
    back-reference its predecessor's design rationale and
    forward-reference at least one upcoming peer.
  - **Adding a body-mutation coverage entry remains a pure-data
    change** when the shape is documented in the embedded
    OpenAPI spec — no production code, registry, schema, or
    manifest edits are needed because the embedded OpenAPI
    document already advertises the operation. The harness's
    `runAPICoverageSuccess` reads `tc.SampleBody` and asserts
    the wire-level body forwarding through the typed JSON
    decoder; no per-case runner logic is needed.

- **Implemented**: Coverage of Dokploy operation `server-setupMonitoring`
  via the data-driven `apiCoverageCase` harness in
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`. Thirteenth entry in the server/*
  roster and the **fourth body-mutation peer** (after API-0338
  `server-create`, API-0344 `server-remove`, API-0346 `server-setup`).
- **Forward-reference correction**: API-0346's design block predicted
  this entry would be a "pure copy-and-rename" mirror of its single-
  required-`serverId` body. The embedded spec
  (`internal/api/data/openapi.json > /server.setupMonitoring > post`)
  proved that wrong: the body declares TWO required top-level fields,
  `serverId` (string) and `metricsConfig` (deeply nested object). The
  required leaves are: `metricsConfig.server.{refreshRate, port,
  token, urlCallback, retentionDays, cronJob, thresholds}` and
  `metricsConfig.server.thresholds.{cpu, memory}` and
  `metricsConfig.containers.{refreshRate, services}`. This is the
  **largest body shape on the server/* mutation roster** — strictly
  larger than API-0338's seven flat required fields. Re-anchors the
  API-0345 lesson that `internal/api/data/openapi.json` is the single
  source of truth.
- **Files changed**:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended API-0347 case at the
    end of `coveredAPIOperations`. Body fixture populates every required
    leaf per the spec.
  - `ralph/prd.json` — set API-0347 `passes: true` (note: stories live
    under top-level `userStories`, NOT a separate `apiStories` array).
- **Verification run**:
  - `gofmt -l .` — clean (no diff).
  - `go mod tidy` — clean.
  - `go vet ./...` — pass.
  - `go test ./...` — all packages `ok`.
  - `go test -race ./...` — all packages `ok`.
  - `go test ./internal/cli/ -run 'TestAPICoverage' -count=1` — pass.
- **Tools NOT available locally** (unchanged baseline): `goimports`,
  `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`, `goreleaser`.
- **Remaining risks / follow-ups**: API-0348 `server-update` is the
  next pending server/* mutation; do NOT blindly mirror API-0347 — its
  `update`-shaped schemas typically declare many optional + a few
  required fields, so re-verify against the embedded spec first.
  API-0349 `server-validate` is a single-required-`serverId` query GET
  and should mirror API-0345 `server-security`. API-0350
  `server-withSSHKey` is unknown until verified.
- **Learnings for future iterations**:
  - **Forward-reference comments lie when the spec disagrees.** The
    API-0345 lesson held: API-0346 promised API-0347 would be "pure
    copy-and-rename"; it turned out to be the largest body in the tag.
    Always re-verify against `internal/api/data/openapi.json` first.
  - **PRD shape gotcha**: all stories (user + API) live under a single
    top-level `userStories` array in `ralph/prd.json`. There is no
    separate `apiStories` array, even though the agent prompt and PRD
    summary text talk about API stories as a category. A naive
    `for s in d['apiStories']` loop will silently no-op and write the
    file unchanged. Use `for s in d['userStories']` and filter by
    `id` prefix or `type == 'api-operation'` if needed.
  - **Body-fixture token literals**: when an OpenAPI body declares a
    `token` field (e.g. `metricsConfig.server.token` here), pick a
    fixture literal that does NOT match the test harness's resolved
    `--token` value (`test-token-value`) and does NOT match any
    `Authorization:` / `X-API-Key:` / `?token=` / `?api_key=`
    redactor pattern, so the round-trip body-equality assertion in
    `runAPICoverageSuccess` is not corrupted by `output.NewRedactor`.
    A `fixture-token-<slug>-<storyID>` shape is safe.
  - **Use IANA-reserved domains for fixture URLs** (`example.invalid`,
    not a real-looking domain) so the literal can never be confused
    with a production callback if it leaks into a log.
  - **Numeric leaves should encode schema intent** (e.g. port 9100 =
    node_exporter default, 80% CPU/memory thresholds, 7-day retention)
    so the fixture documents the field's purpose at a glance for the
    next reviewer.
  - **No 404 on server/* mutations stays load-bearing**: the per-tag
    convention reasserted at API-0335..API-0346 holds again here. Body
    mutations on the server/* tag enumerate 200/400/401/403/500 only;
    404 stays reserved for the by-id GET peer (API-0342 `server-one`).
    Failure-leg fields stay omitted; harness defaults (401 → CodeAuth)
    apply.

  - The `*-update` body shape for a tag is reliably "the tag's `*-create`
    body **plus** a new required record-id field" — useful template
    intuition, but the API-0345 / API-0347 forward-reference rule still
    requires re-verifying against `internal/api/data/openapi.json` because
    optional fields and nullables can drift between create and update peers
    (here, `command` is added on update only).

    composes cleanly with the API-0292 / API-0297 / API-0338
    "populated-string for anyOf [string, null]" rule — populating both
    optionals AND nullables maximises wire-forwarding coverage in a single
    fixture.

    increment within RFC 5737 (`192.0.2.0/24`) so each peer's wire payload
    stays diff-distinguishable from prior peers (API-0338 used
    `192.0.2.10`; this story used `192.0.2.20`).
  - `enum` literal choice for shared scalars (`serverType` `["deploy",
    "build"]`) should match the prior peer's literal — API-0338 pinned
    `"deploy"` and explicitly forward-referenced `server-update`, so this
    story matches verbatim.
  - The repo's apiCoverageCase harness is the canonical add-an-API
    extension point: a single entry append covers registry, schema,
    manifest, success, and failure contract assertions with no per-story
    test file required. Resist the urge to add bespoke tests unless a
    story genuinely needs richer assertions than the harness offers
    (the harness comment at line ~5159 explicitly anticipates this).

    `/server.security` modulo `operationId`. The `jq -S … | del(.operationId)`

    should become standard practice for any "verbatim mirror"

    and prevents the API-0345 / API-0347 / API-0348-class mistakes.

    point for the final server/* peer (API-0350).

    to be the cheapest "future me" guidance — the API-0345 entry's
    explicit "API-0349 should mirror this verbatim" line saved
    re-deriving the shape from scratch and was confirmed by the
    spec diff above. Maintaining this discipline keeps the harness
    truly append-only.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0350 - Cover server-withSSHKey (GET /server.withSSHKey)
- Added `coveredAPIOperations` entry for `server-withSSHKey` in
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`, closing out the server/* tag
  (entries API-0335..API-0350 now complete).
- The spec is a parameter-free GET (zero query, zero path, zero body),
  byte-identical to `/ai.getAll > get` modulo the `tag` array. Verified
  via `diff <(jq -S '.paths["/ai.getAll"].get | del(.operationId)' …)
  <(jq -S '.paths["/server.withSSHKey"].get | del(.operationId)' …)` —
  single-line diff on `tag` only. Inherits the parameter-free GET wire
  shape from API-0006 `ai-getAll` (rather than mirroring API-0349
  `server-validate`'s single-required-query-param shape, which the
  forward-reference comment correctly flagged as "unknown until
  re-verified"). No SampleQuery, SamplePathParams, or SampleBody —
  matches the no-input agent-contract shape
  `yalla api call server-withSSHKey --input '{}' --json`.
- Files changed:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` (+ ~95 lines of new entry +
    inline narrative; no other code touched).
  - `ralph/prd.json` (`API-0350.passes` flipped to `true`).
  - `ralph/progress.txt` (this entry).
- Verification run:
  - `gofmt -w .` — clean.
  - `go mod tidy` — clean.
  - `go vet ./...` — clean.
  - `go test ./...` — pass.
  - `go test -race ./...` — pass (cli pkg ran with -race in 2.6s).
- Results: All required quality gates green. Failure-leg covered by the
  harness default (401 → CodeAuth) per the per-tag opener convention
  reasserted at API-0335..API-0349; 404 → CodeNotFound stays reserved
  for the canonical by-id `server-one` peer (API-0342).
- Remaining risks or follow-ups:
  - Server/* tag is now closed (16 stories complete). The next pending
    case in the PRD roster is API-0351 `settings-assignDomainServer`,
    which opens the settings/* tag and must introduce a fresh
    `set-cov-*` per-tag fixture-isolation namespace orthogonal to the
    `srv-cov-*` namespace established at API-0335. Per the
    forward-reference lesson, do NOT mirror API-0350's shape blindly:
    the settings/* opener must re-verify its spec against
    `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.assignDomainServer`
    before authoring.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **Always re-verify spec before mirroring.** API-0349's
    forward-reference comment correctly flagged that API-0350's shape
    was "unknown until re-verified," and the verification turned up
    a different shape (parameter-free GET, not single-required-query-
    param). Blindly copying API-0349's `SampleQuery: serverId` would
    have produced a runtime error when the harness POSTed an
    unexpected query parameter against the parameter-free spec.
  - **Parameter-free GET cohort lineage.** When an operation has zero
    parameters and no request body, mirror the API-0006 `ai-getAll`
    cohort (continued by API-0098 `deployment-allCentralized`,
    API-0246 `organization-active`, etc.): leave `SampleQuery`,
    `SamplePathParams`, and `SampleBody` all unset and rely on
    `runAPICoverageSuccess`'s wire-level invariants (method, path,
    Authorization header, empty query string, empty body).
  - **Tag-closing entries should forward-reference the next tag's
    opener.** API-0350 closes server/* and forward-references
    API-0351 `settings-assignDomainServer` (the settings/* opener),
    flagging the per-tag fixture-isolation namespace transition
    (`srv-cov-*` → `set-cov-*`) so the next contributor doesn't
    accidentally back-reference the closed tag's namespace.
  - **`diff <(jq -S '.paths[…].get | del(.operationId)' …)` is the
    canonical spec-equivalence check.** Stripping the `operationId`
    field lets you diff the structural shape (parameters, request
    body, responses) without the unique-per-operation key creating
    noise. Mention the byte-identical comparison in the inline
    narrative so future readers can verify with the same one-liner.
