## 2026-05-08 - API-0351 — Cover settings-assignDomainServer (POST /settings.assignDomainServer)
- **Implemented**: Added the canonical `apiCoverageCase` entry for
  `settings-assignDomainServer` (POST /settings.assignDomainServer, tag
  `settings`) to `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`. This is the **first**
  settings/* story; it **opens the settings/* tag** and establishes the
  `set-cov-*` per-tag fixture-isolation namespace orthogonal to every
  prior tag's namespace (`srv-cov-*`, `proj-cov-*`, `org-cov-*`,
  `compose-cov-*`, `app-cov-*`, `ai-cov-*`, etc.). The shared harness
  (`TestAPICoverage_RegistryInvariants`, `_SchemaCommandWorks`,
  `_ManifestListsOperation`, `_SuccessRoundTrip`,
  `_RepresentativeFailure`) now drives registry, schema, manifest,
  success, and 401 → CodeAuth failure assertions for this operation
  automatically.
- Spec re-verified against
  `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.assignDomainServer > post`
  per the API-0345..API-0350 forward-reference lesson:
  requestBody.required = true with object schema declaring four
  properties — `host` (string, required), `certificateType` (enum
  `letsencrypt|none|custom`, required), `letsEncryptEmail` (anyOf
  string-or-empty-string-or-null, optional), `https` (boolean,
  optional). Zero parameters. Responses 200/400/401/403/500 — note
  the **absence of 404** (settings is a singleton, not a by-id
  resource). 200 schema is `{}` with `additionalProperties: false`.
- Fixture choices documented inline:
  - `host`: `set-cov-assignDomainServer-0351.example.test` (DNS-shaped slug).
  - `certificateType`: `"none"` (safest enum member; avoids implying
    cert-provisioning side effects).
  - `letsEncryptEmail`: `set-cov-assignDomainServer-0351@example.test`
    (deterministic-but-fake; populating optionals exercises the JSON
    serialiser's optional-field branch, per the API-0004 convention).
  - `https`: `false` (matches the `"none"` certificate choice).
- **Files changed**:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended the API-0351 case
    after the API-0350 entry. The block forward-references API-0352
    `settings-checkGPUStatus` (the next pending settings/* peer, a
    parameter-free GET shaped like API-0006 `ai-getAll` /
    API-0350 `server-withSSHKey`, NOT this POST-with-body opener),
    flagging the namespace-inheritance rule and the spec
    re-verification requirement.
  - `ralph/prd.json` — set API-0351 `passes: true`.
  - `ralph/progress.txt` — this entry.
- **Verification run** from repo root:
  - `gofmt -w .` — clean.
  - `go mod tidy` — clean.
  - `go vet ./...` — clean.
  - `go test ./...` — all packages pass.
  - `go test -race ./...` — all packages pass (`internal/cli` 2.601s).
- Tools NOT installed locally (per Codebase Patterns): `goimports`,
  `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`, `goreleaser`. Skipped
  silently per the existing convention; the file already documents this.
- Remaining risks / follow-ups: none specific to API-0351. The next
  pending settings/* story is API-0352 `settings-checkGPUStatus`. Per
  the forward-reference lesson, do NOT mirror this POST-with-body
  opener's shape — re-verify against
  `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.checkGPUStatus > get` and
  inherit the `set-cov-*` namespace established here.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **Tag-opening entries establish a fresh per-tag fixture-isolation
    namespace.** API-0351 opens settings/* and establishes
    `set-cov-*` orthogonal to all prior namespaces. The pattern is
    documented in two places (the new entry's comment block and the
    Codebase Patterns section's namespace-isolation rule reasserted
    at API-0335..API-0350) so future tag-openers can copy the
    convention verbatim without spelunking through 5,000+ lines of
    test fixtures.
  - **The settings tag has no 404 response** because settings is a
    singleton, not a by-id resource. This is structurally different
    from server/*, project/*, organization/*, etc., which all have
    `*-one` peers. The 404 → CodeNotFound override stays reserved
    for those `*-one` peers; settings/* peers (this one and future
    API-0352..) all default to 401 → CodeAuth via the harness.
  - **Optional field population follows the API-0004 `ai-deploy`
    convention.** Even when the spec marks a field optional
    (`letsEncryptEmail`, `https`), populating it with a
    deterministic-but-clearly-fake value exercises the JSON
    serialiser's optional-field branch on the wire and gives future
    schema-validator wiring more surface area to assert against.
  - **Enum-typed required fields prefer the safest member.**
    `certificateType` is set to `"none"` rather than `"letsencrypt"`
    or `"custom"` because the latter two imply cert-provisioning
    side effects the fixture cannot honour. This generalises: when
    a required enum has a "no-op" member, pick it.
---

## 2026-05-08 - API-0352 — Cover settings-checkGPUStatus (GET /settings.checkGPUStatus)
- **Implemented**: Added the canonical `apiCoverageCase` entry for
  `settings-checkGPUStatus` (GET /settings.checkGPUStatus, tag
  `settings`) to `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`. This is the
  **second** settings/* story; it inherits the `set-cov-*` per-tag
  fixture-isolation namespace established at API-0351
  `settings-assignDomainServer` (must not back-reference the closed
  `srv-cov-*`, `proj-cov-*`, `org-cov-*`, `compose-cov-*`,
  `app-cov-*`, `ai-cov-*`, etc. namespaces). The shared harness
  (`TestAPICoverage_RegistryInvariants`, `_SchemaCommandWorks`,
  `_ManifestListsOperation`, `_SuccessRoundTrip`,
  `_RepresentativeFailure`) drives registry, schema, manifest,
  success, and 401 → CodeAuth failure assertions for this operation
  automatically; all five subtests pass for `API-0352`.
- Spec re-verified against
  `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.checkGPUStatus > get`
  per the API-0345..API-0351 forward-reference lesson:
  zero request body, a single **OPTIONAL** query parameter
  `serverId` (string, **no `required: true`**), no path parameters.
  Responses 200/400/401/403/404/500. Structurally byte-identical to
  `/server.validate > get` modulo (a) the `tag` array and (b) the
  `serverId` `required` flag, verified at story authoring time via
  `diff <(jq -S '.paths["/server.validate"].get | del(.operationId)' …)
  <(jq -S '.paths["/settings.checkGPUStatus"].get | del(.operationId)' …)`,
  which produced exactly two-line drift. The 200 schema is `{}`
  with `additionalProperties: false`, matching every prior covered
  peer.
- **Forward-reference correction surfaced.** API-0351's comment had
  predicted API-0352 would be a parameter-free GET (cf. API-0006
  `ai-getAll`, API-0350 `server-withSSHKey`). The re-verification
  turned up a single-OPTIONAL-query-param shape instead — closer to
  API-0349 `server-validate` but with the `required` constraint
  relaxed. This is the latest iteration of the "always re-verify the
  spec before mirroring" lesson (API-0345/0347/0348/0349/0350 each
  surfaced the same correction). The new entry's "Forward-reference
  correction" comment block records this in-place so future
  contributors see the trail.
- Fixture decisions:
  - `serverId`: `"set-cov-checkGPUStatus-0352"` (per-case fixture
    token, deterministic-but-clearly-fake; populating the optional
    parameter exercises the query-string wire path through the raw
    API executor and keeps this case distinct from the parameter-
    free GET cohort).
- **Files changed**:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended the API-0352 case
    after the API-0351 entry. The block forward-references API-0353
    `settings-cleanAll` (the next pending settings/* peer, a POST
    with `requestBody.required = false` and an optional `serverId`
    body field — materially different shape, NOT a copy-and-rename
    of this single-query-param GET), flagging the namespace-
    inheritance rule and the spec re-verification requirement.
  - `ralph/prd.json` — set API-0352 `passes: true`.
  - `ralph/progress.txt` — this entry.
- **Verification run** from repo root:
  - `gofmt -w .` — clean.
  - `go mod tidy` — clean.
  - `go vet ./...` — clean.
  - `go test ./...` — all packages pass.
  - `go test -race ./...` — all packages pass (`internal/cli` 2.606s).
  - `go test ./internal/cli/ -run 'TestAPICoverage_.*/API-0352' -v` —
    all five harness subtests pass for API-0352.
- Tools NOT installed locally (per Codebase Patterns): `goimports`,
  `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`, `goreleaser`. Skipped
  silently per the existing convention; `internal/cli/AGENTS.md`
  documents this.
- Remaining risks / follow-ups: none specific to API-0352. The next
  pending settings/* story is API-0353 `settings-cleanAll`. Per the
  forward-reference lesson, do NOT mirror this single-query-param GET
  shape — re-verify against
  `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.cleanAll > post` and
  inherit the `set-cov-*` namespace established at API-0351.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **The `required` flag is metadata, not wire shape.** OpenAPI's
    `required: true` on a parameter only tells the client whether
    pre-flight validation should reject a missing input — it does not
    change the HTTP handshake when the parameter IS supplied. So
    `/server.validate` (required serverId) and
    `/settings.checkGPUStatus` (optional serverId) are byte-identical
    on the wire when both fixtures populate `serverId`. The harness's
    success-leg round trip exercises the populated path either way;
    the distinction matters only for human contributors deciding
    whether to populate the fixture (always populate for query
    parameters — leaving them unset would degrade the case to the
    parameter-free GET cohort and lose the query-forwarding wire
    assertion).
  - **Forward-reference predictions can be wrong; the comments must
    record both the prediction AND the correction.** API-0351
    confidently predicted "parameter-free GET" but the spec showed a
    single-optional-query-param GET. The new entry documents the
    correction inline in a "Forward-reference correction" block so
    the next contributor sees both the original guess and the
    verified shape — this preserves the lesson without erasing the
    history of how it was found.
  - **`apiCoverageCase` literals use 2-tab indentation, NOT 3.**
    A file-bytes inspection (`od -c`) confirmed each case body
    indents fields at `\t\t`, the closing `},` at `\t`, and the
    slice's outer `}` is flush left. An earlier Edit attempt with
    3-tab indentation failed to match because the Read tool's
    line-number prefix can visually inflate apparent indentation
    depth — when in doubt, run
    `awk 'NR>=L1 && NR<=L2' FILE | od -c` to confirm raw bytes
    before authoring the Edit `old_string`.
---

## 2026-05-08 - API-0353 — Cover settings-cleanAll (POST /settings.cleanAll)
- **Implemented**: Added the canonical `apiCoverageCase` entry for
  `settings-cleanAll` (POST /settings.cleanAll, tag `settings`) to
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`. This is the **third** settings/*
  story; it inherits the `set-cov-*` per-tag fixture-isolation namespace
  established at API-0351 `settings-assignDomainServer` (must not
  back-reference the closed `srv-cov-*`, `proj-cov-*`, `org-cov-*`,
  `compose-cov-*`, `app-cov-*`, `ai-cov-*`, etc. namespaces). The shared
  harness (`TestAPICoverage_RegistryInvariants`, `_SchemaCommandWorks`,
  `_ManifestListsOperation`, `_SuccessRoundTrip`,
  `_RepresentativeFailure`) drives registry, schema, manifest, success,
  and 401 → CodeAuth failure assertions for this operation
  automatically; all five subtests pass for `API-0353`.
- Spec re-verified against
  `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.cleanAll > post` per the
  API-0345..API-0352 forward-reference lesson:
  * `requestBody.required = false` carrying a JSON object with a single
    optional string property `serverId` and **no** `required` array.
  * Zero parameters (no query, no path, no header).
  * Responses 200/400/401/403/500 — note the **absence of 404**,
    mirroring the API-0351 opener's response set rather than API-0352's
    by-id-flavoured 404-bearing response set.
  * 200 schema is `{}` with `additionalProperties: false`, matching
    every prior covered peer.
- Forward-reference correction: API-0352's comment correctly predicted
  this entry's POST-with-optional-body shape (the optional-body /
  optional-`serverId` schema vs. API-0352's optional-query-`serverId`
  schema); the prediction held this time, but the spec was still
  re-verified against the embedded openapi.json before the fixture was
  authored. The harness's wire assertions confirm body forwarding,
  Content-Type, and method/path round-trip.
- Fixture choices:
  * Per-case fixture token base `set-cov-cleanAll-0353` follows the
    `<tag>-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` convention shared across every per-tag
    roster; verified unique vs. API-0351 `set-cov-assignDomainServer-0351`
    and API-0352 `set-cov-checkGPUStatus-0352`.
  * `serverId` body field carries the deterministic-but-clearly-fake
    literal `set-cov-cleanAll-0353` rather than a real UUID. Even
    though both the body and the field are optional, populating both
    keeps the wire round-trip distinguishable from the
    parameter-free POST cohort and exercises the JSON serialiser's
    optional-body branch on the wire.
- **Files changed**:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended the API-0353 case
    to `coveredAPIOperations`.
  - `ralph/prd.json` — set `passes: true` for API-0353.
- **Verification run**:
  - `gofmt -w .` — clean.
  - `go mod tidy` — clean (no dep changes).
  - `go vet ./...` — clean.
  - `go test ./...` — all packages `ok`.
  - `go test -race ./...` — all packages `ok`.
- **Tools NOT available locally** (unchanged from earlier stories):
  `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`,
  `goreleaser`.
- **Remaining risks / follow-ups**:
  - The next case in the settings/* roster, API-0354
    `settings-cleanAllDeploymentQueue`, is also a POST per the verb's
    `clean*` family pattern. Authors must not assume the body shape
    mirrors this entry's `{serverId}` schema — re-verify the spec
    against
    `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.cleanAllDeploymentQueue > post`
    per the forward-reference lesson.
- **Learnings for future iterations**:
  - When a POST declares `requestBody.required = false`, the canonical
    fixture choice is to populate the body anyway. Leaving the body
    unset would degrade the case to the parameter-free POST cohort
    (no Content-Type assertion) and lose the body-forwarding round
    trip that distinguishes this shape from query-only or path-only
    siblings. Populating the body keeps the harness's success-leg
    `Content-Type: application/json` and JSON-equality body
    assertions meaningful.
  - The fixture-token convention `<tag>-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` is
    collision-safe across tags because the tag prefix is mandatory;
    verify against neighbouring tag rosters (no `srv-cov-*`,
    `proj-cov-*`, `org-cov-*`, `compose-cov-*`, `app-cov-*`,
    `ai-cov-*` reuse) when adding the first few entries of a new tag,
    then trust the prefix for subsequent peers in the same tag.
  - Optional-body POSTs whose response set lacks 404 are common in
    fleet-wide / scope-by-optional-id verbs (`cleanAll`,
    `cleanAllDeploymentQueue`, `cleanDockerBuilder`, …). The per-tag
    opener convention that reserves 404 → CodeNotFound for `*-one`
    by-id peers does not even apply — falling through to the harness
    default 401 → CodeAuth is the right call.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0354 — Cover settings-cleanAllDeploymentQueue (POST /settings.cleanAllDeploymentQueue)
- **Implemented**: Added the canonical `apiCoverageCase` entry for
  `settings-cleanAllDeploymentQueue` (POST
  /settings.cleanAllDeploymentQueue, tag `settings`) to
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`. This is the **fourth** settings/*
  story; it inherits the `set-cov-*` per-tag fixture-isolation namespace
  established at API-0351 `settings-assignDomainServer` (must not
  back-reference any closed `srv-cov-*`, `proj-cov-*`, `org-cov-*`,
  `compose-cov-*`, `app-cov-*`, `ai-cov-*`, etc. namespace). The shared
  harness (`TestAPICoverage_RegistryInvariants`, `_SchemaCommandWorks`,
  `_ManifestListsOperation`, `_SuccessRoundTrip`,
  `_RepresentativeFailure`) drives registry, schema, manifest, success,
  and 401 → CodeAuth failure assertions for this operation
  automatically; all five subtests pass for `API-0354`.
- Spec re-verified against `internal/api/data/openapi.json >
  /settings.cleanAllDeploymentQueue > post` per the API-0345..API-0353
  forward-reference lesson:
  * **No `requestBody` field at all** in the spec — this is
    structurally stronger than API-0353 `settings-cleanAll`'s
    `requestBody.required = false` carrying an optional `serverId`.
    Here the operation declares no request body schema whatsoever.
  * Zero parameters (no query, no path, no header).
  * Responses 200/400/401/403/500 — note the **absence of 404**,
    mirroring the API-0351 opener's and API-0353's response sets
    rather than API-0352's by-id-flavoured 404-bearing response set.
  * 200 schema is `{}` with `additionalProperties: false`, matching
    every prior covered peer.
- **Forward-reference correction**: API-0353's comment predicted this
  entry would be a POST per the `clean*`-family verb pattern but
  cautioned against assuming the body shape mirrored API-0353's
  `{serverId}` schema. Re-verification confirmed the caution: this
  operation has *no* request body at all, degenerating from
  API-0353's optional-body shape to a true no-input POST. This is a
  brand-new shape on the coverage corpus — the **first** parameter-
  free, no-request-body POST entry across all `coveredAPIOperations`
  (a programmatic scan for prior `MethodPost` blocks lacking
  `SampleBody`, `SampleQuery`, and `SamplePathParams` returned this
  entry alone).
- Fixture choices:
  * Per-case fixture token base `set-cov-cleanAllDeploymentQueue-0354`
    follows the `<tag>-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` convention shared across
    every per-tag roster; verified unique vs. API-0351
    `set-cov-assignDomainServer-0351`, API-0352
    `set-cov-checkGPUStatus-0352`, and API-0353
    `set-cov-cleanAll-0353`. Even though no fixture-token literal is
    materialised on the wire (the operation has no body and no
    parameters), the slug is reserved here to keep the per-tag
    cross-reference grep useful for future contributors.
  * **Neither `SampleBody`, `SampleQuery`, nor `SamplePathParams` are
    populated.** The spec declares no body and no parameters, and
    inventing a fictional body would (a) violate the
    re-verify-the-spec rule reasserted across API-0345..API-0353,
    (b) cause `runAPICoverageSuccess` to assert a body round-trip
    the CLI would never send, and (c) waste the no-body branch
    coverage this entry uniquely provides.
- **Files changed**:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended the API-0354 case
    to `coveredAPIOperations`.
  - `ralph/prd.json` — set `passes: true` for API-0354.
  - `ralph/progress.txt` — this entry.
- **Verification run** from repo root:
  - `gofmt -w .` — clean.
  - `go mod tidy` — clean (no dep changes).
  - `go vet ./...` — clean.
  - `go test ./...` — all packages `ok`.
  - `go test -race ./...` — all packages `ok` (`internal/cli` 2.605s).
  - `go test -run TestAPICoverage ./internal/cli/ -count=1 -v` — all
    five `API-0354/settings-cleanAllDeploymentQueue` subtests PASS
    (Registry, Schema, Manifest, Success, RepresentativeFailure).
- **Tools NOT available locally** (per Codebase Patterns):
  `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`,
  `goreleaser`. Skipped silently per the existing convention.
- **Remaining risks / follow-ups**: none specific to API-0354. The
  next pending settings/* story is API-0355
  `settings-cleanDockerBuilder`. Per the forward-reference lesson, do
  NOT mirror this entry's no-body shape, API-0353's `{serverId}`
  shape, or API-0351's `{host,certificateType,...}` shape —
  re-verify against `internal/api/data/openapi.json >
  /settings.cleanDockerBuilder > post` before authoring. The
  `clean*` verb family in the settings/* tag has already shown
  three different body shapes (none, optional `{serverId}`, and the
  upcoming API-0355 unknown), proving the per-operation spec is the
  only authoritative source.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **No-body POST is a distinct sub-cohort.** Until this story, every
    POST entry in `coveredAPIOperations` had a `SampleBody`. API-0354
    establishes the no-body-POST convention: omit `SampleBody`,
    `SampleQuery`, and `SamplePathParams` together; the harness's
    `if len(tc.SampleBody) > 0` guard at `runAPICoverageSuccess`
    skips the Content-Type and body round-trip assertions, while
    method, path, Authorization header, empty query string,
    schema_version, data.operation_id, data.method, and data.status
    all remain asserted. This is the canonical form for a future
    `clean*`-family no-body peer.
  - **`requestBody.required = false` ≠ no `requestBody`.** API-0353
    had an optional body schema (`requestBody.required = false`
    carrying `{serverId}`); API-0354 has no `requestBody` field at
    all. The two shapes both fall under the same agent contract
    (`yalla api call <op> --input '{}' --json` works for both), but
    on the wire only the API-0353 case forwards a body, and only
    API-0353's success-leg test asserts a body round-trip.
    Re-verifying the spec against the embedded openapi.json before
    authoring is the only way to disambiguate — this is the
    seventh consecutive iteration of the same lesson
    (API-0345/0347/0348/0349/0350/0352/0354).
  - **Fixture-slug reservation is still useful for no-body POSTs.**
    Even when no fixture-token literal reaches the wire, reserving
    `set-cov-<slug>-<storyID>` keeps future contributors' cross-
    reference grep working — a contributor authoring API-0355 can
    `rg 'set-cov-cleanAllDeploymentQueue'` and find this entry as
    the canonical no-body POST exemplar.

    body-only operations terse — no need to populate `SampleQuery` or
    `SamplePathParams` when the spec declares no parameters.

    the lowercased operation token — `Postgres` → `postgres`. So
    `backup-cov-manual-postgres-0046` is the canonical sample id.
  - The PRD flip uses a targeted regex within the story's segment
    (between `"id": "API-0046"` and the next `"id":`). This avoids
    accidental matches on other stories' `"passes": false` lines and
    leaves the file's whitespace exactly as upstream wrote it (no
    line-count delta).

- **Implemented**: Added the canonical `apiCoverageCase` entry for

  `settings-assignDomainServer`. The shared harness
  (`TestAPICoverage_RegistryInvariants`, `_SchemaCommandWorks`,
  `_ManifestListsOperation`, `_SuccessRoundTrip`,
  `_RepresentativeFailure`) drives registry, schema, manifest, success,
  and 401 → CodeAuth failure assertions for this operation
  automatically; all five subtests pass for `API-0355`.
- Spec re-verified against `internal/api/data/openapi.json >
  /settings.cleanDockerBuilder > post`:
  * `requestBody.required = false`, single OPTIONAL `serverId` (string),
    no `required` array — byte-identical to API-0353 `settings-cleanAll`
    (NOT API-0354 `settings-cleanAllDeploymentQueue`'s no-body shape).
  * Zero parameters (no query, no path, no header).
  * Responses 200/400/401/403/500 — same set as API-0351/0353/0354;
    no 404 (this is a fleet-wide operation, not by-id).
  * 200 schema is `{}` with `additionalProperties: false`, matching
    every prior covered peer.
- **Forward-reference correction**: API-0354's progress entry cautioned
  that this entry's body shape should NOT be assumed to mirror its own
  no-body schema, API-0353's `{serverId}` schema, or API-0351's
  `{host,certificateType,...}` schema. Re-verification confirmed the
  spec here is byte-identical to API-0353 — optional body carrying an
  optional `serverId`. The `clean*` family in settings/* therefore
  splits into two sub-cohorts on the wire: (a) optional-body POSTs
  with optional `serverId` (API-0353 cleanAll, API-0355
  cleanDockerBuilder, API-0356 cleanDockerPrune per direct spec
  inspection), and (b) no-body POSTs (API-0354
  cleanAllDeploymentQueue, API-0357 cleanMonitoring, API-0358
  cleanRedis, API-0359 cleanSSHPrivateKey per direct spec inspection).
- **Files changed**:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended one
    `apiCoverageCase` literal at the tail of `coveredAPIOperations`.
  - `ralph/prd.json` — flipped `passes: false` → `true` for API-0355.
  - `ralph/progress.txt` — this entry.
- **Verification run** (from repo root):
  - `gofmt -w .` — no diff after format.
  - `go mod tidy` — no dep changes.
  - `go vet ./...` — clean.
  - `go test ./...` — all packages `ok`.
  - `go test -race ./...` — all packages `ok` (`internal/cli` 2.653s).
  - `go test -run TestAPICoverage ./internal/cli/ -count=1 -v` — all
    five `API-0355/settings-cleanDockerBuilder` subtests PASS
    (Registry, Schema, Manifest, Success, RepresentativeFailure).
- **Tools NOT available locally** (per Codebase Patterns): `goimports`,
  `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`, `goreleaser`. Skipped
  silently per the existing convention.
- **Remaining risks / follow-ups**: none specific to API-0355. The next
  pending settings/* story is API-0356 `settings-cleanDockerPrune`.
  Per direct spec inspection during this iteration, API-0356 carries
  the same optional-body `{serverId}` shape as this entry — but per
  the forward-reference lesson, the next contributor MUST still
  re-verify the spec before authoring the fixture. The verb prefix
  `clean*` alone is not authoritative (API-0354 already proved two
  `clean*` siblings can diverge on the body axis).
- **Learnings for future iterations**:
  - The settings/* `clean*` family does NOT have a single canonical
    body shape. Two sub-cohorts coexist: optional-body `{serverId}`
    (API-0353/0355/0356) and no-body (API-0354/0357/0358/0359). The
    OpenAPI spec is the only authority — `requestBody` either has
    `content.application/json.schema` or it is absent entirely. Both
    are valid in the harness; the `if len(tc.SampleBody) > 0` guard
    handles each correctly.
  - For optional-body POSTs the canonical fixture is to populate the
    body with the per-case slug (`set-cov-cleanDockerBuilder-0355` in
    this entry). Even though the body and the `serverId` field are
    both optional, populating them keeps the success-leg
    `Content-Type: application/json` header assertion meaningful and
    distinguishes this shape from the no-body POST cohort.
  - Forward-reference predictions in comment blocks should now
    explicitly call out the *axis of divergence* (e.g. "do not assume
    the BODY shape mirrors") rather than a generic "do not assume
    similarity" — API-0354's caution was specific enough that
    authoring this entry took ~5 minutes of spec re-verification, vs
    open-ended cautions which require re-reading the entire spec.

The lesson: forward references are pointers to a *category* (here: query-only GET with no body), not a guarantee of byte-identical fixtures. Always re-verify against `data/openapi.json` even when the previous story's comment confidently predicts the shape.

Future backup/* stories outside the manualBackup family should use the general namespace; the `manual` infix is reserved for that closed cohort.

  - The OpenAPI 200 schema being `{}` with `additionalProperties: false` is *not* the same as the runtime payload — Dokploy returns the populated record on 200; the spec just leaves the response surface empty. The coverage harness asserts forwarding and envelope shape, not payload semantics, so the empty-object `SuccessResponse` fixture is correct (and matches every prior covered peer's convention).

  API-0051+ (bitbucket/*) per the priority order in `ralph/prd.json`.

- The `apiCoverageCase` struct fields are minimal: leaving
  SuccessStatus/SuccessResponse/FailureStatus/FailureCode at zero values
  picks up sane defaults (200, `{}`, 401, CodeAuth). For an empty-body
  POST with a single required string field and the standard
  400/401/403/500 response set, the entry needs only StoryID,
  OperationID, Method, Path, Tag, SampleBody, and (optionally)

  the manual sub-namespace for non-manualBackup stories would break the
  cross-story grep convention.

  enforces this). Inserting between API-0048 and API-0066 keeps the
  diff a single contiguous insert and the file order matches the
  PRD's natural enumeration order.
- The `TestAPICoverage_*` family auto-discovers entries from
  `coveredAPIOperations`, so adding a new fixture is the ONLY change
  required — no test-function edits, no registry edits, no schema
  fixture files. The five subtests (Registry, Schema, Manifest,
  Success, Failure) get generated automatically.

    200/400/401/403/500. Fixture sets only StoryID/OperationID/Method/

  - `gofmt -w .` — clean.
  - `go mod tidy` — clean.
  - `go vet ./...` — clean.
  - `go test ./... -count=1` — all packages `ok`.

- **Remaining risks / follow-ups**: none. settings/* roster continues with
  API-0358 cleanRedis, also a no-body POST per direct spec inspection.
- **Learnings for future iterations**:
  - The settings/* `clean*` family splits into two stable wire sub-cohorts:
    optional-body (API-0353 cleanAll, API-0355 cleanDockerBuilder, API-0356
    cleanDockerPrune) and no-body (API-0354 cleanAllDeploymentQueue,
    API-0357 cleanMonitoring; API-0358/cleanRedis and API-0359/
    cleanSSHPrivateKey are also no-body per spec). The verb prefix alone
    is not authoritative — always re-verify against
    `internal/api/data/openapi.json`.
  - For a no-body POST whose only declared failures are 400/401/403/500,
    the entry needs five fields plus narration: StoryID, OperationID,
    Method, Path, Tag, plus optional SuccessResponse — leaving all body/
    query/path-param fields zero hits the harness's no-body branch
    (`runAPICoverageSuccess` skips Content-Type and body round-trip
    assertions when `len(tc.SampleBody) == 0`) and the harness default
    `tc.FailureStatus == 0` → 401, `tc.FailureCode == ""` → CodeAuth
    delivers the canonical failure-leg mapping with no extra config.
  - The per-tag fixture-isolation namespace (here `set-cov-*`) is
    reserved for the slug of every entry on the roster even when no
    fixture-token literal lands on the wire — keeps cross-story grep
    convention working for future contributors.

    optional-body members (API-0353 cleanAll, API-0355

    declares a *distinguishing* failure mode (e.g. by-id 404, payload
    400 as the canonical-failure-by-design); generic
    400/401/403/500 sets degrade to the harness default.
  - Cross-story comment forward references continue to pay off: the
    "API-0358 cleanRedis is also a no-body POST" prediction in API-0357
    let this entry's narration cite "Forward-reference confirmation"
    instead of re-authoring the whole sub-cohort taxonomy from scratch.
    Future entries should keep predicting the next peer's body shape so
    the chain self-documents the verb-prefix-is-not-authoritative
    lesson without copy-pasting.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0360 - Cover settings-cleanStoppedContainers (POST /settings.cleanStoppedContainers)
- Added the API-0360 case to `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`'s
  `coveredAPIOperations` table. The new entry registers the operation under
  the settings/* per-tag fixture-isolation namespace seeded by API-0351
  `settings-assignDomainServer`, with operationId `settings-cleanStoppedContainers`,
  method `POST`, path `/settings.cleanStoppedContainers`, tag `settings`.
- **Spec re-verification per the API-0345..API-0359 forward-reference lesson.**
  Direct inspection of `internal/api/data/openapi.json >
  /settings.cleanStoppedContainers > post` confirms an **OPTIONAL**
  `requestBody` (`requestBody.required = false`) carrying a single optional
  property `serverId: string` with no `required` array; **zero parameters**;
  responses 200/400/401/403/500 (no 404). The 200 schema is `{}` with
  `additionalProperties: false`, matching every prior covered peer.
- Wire shape lands in the **optional-body POST sub-cohort** alongside
  API-0353 `settings-cleanAll`, API-0355 `settings-cleanDockerBuilder`,
  and API-0356 `settings-cleanDockerPrune` — *not* the no-body sub-cohort
  occupied by API-0354 `settings-cleanAllDeploymentQueue`, API-0357
  `settings-cleanMonitoring`, API-0358 `settings-cleanRedis`, and API-0359
  `settings-cleanSSHPrivateKey`. API-0359 → API-0360 is the **fourth
  body-axis flip across the settings/* `clean*` sub-roster**, vindicating
  the API-0359 forward-reference comment that warned the next entry must
  re-verify the spec rather than mirror its no-body schema.
- `SampleBody` populates the optional `serverId` with the fixture-shaped
  literal `set-cov-cleanStoppedContainers-0360` so the wire payload cannot
  be mistaken for a real production server identifier and the success-leg
  `Content-Type: application/json` header assertion stays meaningful.
  No `SampleQuery` / `SamplePathParams` (the operation has zero parameters).
- Failure-leg fields are intentionally omitted: spec declares no 404, and
  the harness defaults (`tc.FailureStatus == 0` → 401, `tc.FailureCode == ""`
  → CodeAuth) cover the canonical auth-failure leg every Dokploy operation
  must re-prove. 400 → CodeInvalidInput remains reserved for stories where
  payload validation is the operation's distinguishing failure mode.
- `SuccessResponse` is the empty-object literal `{}`, matching the spec's
  200 schema (`type: object`, `additionalProperties: false`, no properties).
- Files changed:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — added API-0360 case (+~135 lines).
  - `ralph/prd.json` — toggled `passes: false → true` for API-0360.
  - `ralph/progress.txt` — this entry.
- Verification (`gofmt -w .`, `go mod tidy`, `go vet ./...`, `go test ./...`,
  `go test -race ./...`) all green. `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`,
  `govulncheck`, and `goreleaser` remain not installed locally and are
  documented as missing rather than silently skipped — same status as
  every prior settings/* coverage story.
- No remaining risks. Coverage harness (`runAPICoverageSuccess` /
  `runAPICoverageFailure`) and registry-invariant assertions
  (`TestAPICoverage_RegistryInvariants`) execute the new entry as part of
  the `t.Run(tc.StoryID + "/" + tc.OperationID, …)` per-case subtest set,
  with no per-tag scaffolding required.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The settings/* `clean*` sub-roster has now flipped on the body axis
    **four times** (cleanAll → cleanAllDeploymentQueue, cleanDockerPrune →
    cleanMonitoring, cleanSSHPrivateKey → cleanStoppedContainers, in
    addition to the earlier cleanAllDeploymentQueue → cleanDockerBuilder
    flip). The verb prefix `clean*` is officially **not authoritative**
    on whether the operation carries an optional `{serverId}` body or no
    body at all — every contributor must re-verify
    `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /<operationId> > post >
    requestBody` before authoring the fixture. API-0361
    `settings-cleanUnusedImages` is the next case in the roster and
    should be assumed neither way until the spec is read.
  - The optional-body cohort fixture pattern is identical across
    API-0353 / API-0355 / API-0356 / API-0360: populate `SampleBody`
    with `{"serverId": "set-cov-<slug>-<storyID>"}` so the JSON
    serialiser's optional-body branch is exercised on the wire and the
    `Content-Type: application/json` header assertion remains
    meaningful. Leaving the body unset would degrade the case to the
    parameter-free POST cohort and lose the body-forwarding round-trip
    assertion that distinguishes the optional-body shape from the
    no-body shape.
  - Test file edits to JSON via `json.dump` strip the trailing newline;
    after writing PRD updates with Python, append a single `\n` so
    `git diff` stays a one-line `passes: false → true` change rather
    than a noisy two-region diff.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0363 settings-getDokployCloudIps (GET /settings.getDokployCloudIps)
- Implemented: appended the API-0363 coverage case to
  `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` as the thirteenth entry on the
  settings/* roster — the first member of the parameter-free GET
  sub-family on this tag, pivoting off the eleven-entry
  `clean*`/`assign*`/POST cohort that dominated settings/* between
  API-0351..API-0362. Spec re-verified against
  `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.getDokployCloudIps >
  get`: a GET with **zero parameters** (no query, no path, no header)
  and **no `requestBody`** field at all (GETs in this OpenAPI
  document never carry one); responses 200/400/401/403/404/500 with
  404 reappearing after eleven 404-absent POST peers. The 200 schema
  is `{}` with `additionalProperties: false`, matching every prior
  covered peer. Wire shape lands in the parameter-free GET cohort
  alongside API-0006 `ai-getAll` and API-0350 `server-withSSHKey`,
  *not* the single-OPTIONAL-query GET cohort (API-0352
  `settings-checkGPUStatus` carried a single optional `serverId`
  query parameter — this entry has none). Case populates neither
  `SampleBody`, `SampleQuery`, nor `SamplePathParams`; the harness's
  GET branch ignores `SampleBody` and asserts that no `Content-Type`
  request header is sent on a GET, so carrying any of the three
  would silently encode dead code on the wire. Failure-leg fields
  intentionally omitted: 404 is declared but the per-tag opener
  convention reserves 404 → CodeNotFound for canonical by-id
  `*-one` peers, not for fleet-wide list-style getters like
  `getDokployCloudIps`. Harness defaults
  (`tc.FailureStatus == 0` → 401, `tc.FailureCode == ""` →
  CodeAuth) deliver the canonical failure-leg mapping.
  `SuccessResponse` is the empty-object literal `{}`, matching the
  spec's 200 schema. Forward-reference comment block points to
  API-0364 `settings-getDokployVersion`, which is also a GET with
  zero parameters per the PRD — but per the per-operation
  re-verification rule, future contributors must not assume
  parameter-list / response-set parity solely because the verb
  prefix and adjacent slug match.
- Files changed:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended API-0363 case
    to the `coveredAPIOperations` slice.
  - `ralph/prd.json` — flipped API-0363 `passes` to `true`.
  - `ralph/progress.txt` — this entry.
- Verification run (from repo root):
  - `gofmt -w .`                           → clean.
  - `goimports -w .`                       → not installed (documented).
  - `go mod tidy`                          → clean (no diff).
  - `go vet ./...`                         → pass.
  - `go test ./...`                        → pass (10 packages ok).
  - `go test -race ./...`                  → pass (10 packages ok).
- Results: all required quality gates green; manifest, registry,
  and per-operation contract tests cover the new entry via the
  existing harness with no per-tag scaffolding required.
- Remaining risks or follow-ups: API-0364
  `settings-getDokployVersion` (GET) is the next story and continues
  the settings/* `get*` GET sub-family opened by this entry. Future
  contributors must re-verify the spec rather than mirror this
  entry's parameter-free shape — even adjacent `getDokploy*` slugs
  are not guaranteed to share parameter or response-set shape per
  the eleven-flip body-axis history of the preceding `clean*`
  POST sub-roster (API-0353..API-0362).
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The settings/* roster has now opened its second sub-family at
    API-0363: the parameter-free GET cohort (one entry so far,
    just `getDokployCloudIps`), distinct from the
    single-OPTIONAL-query GET cohort opened at API-0352
    (`checkGPUStatus`) and the eleven-entry `clean*`/`assign*`
    POST cohort (API-0351, API-0353..API-0362). Three GET
    sub-shapes are now possible going forward in settings/*:
    parameter-free, single-optional-query, and (potentially)
    multi-parameter — each must be detected per-operation.
  - The harness's GET branch has three structural guarantees
    that POST handlers never assert: (a) no `Content-Type`
    request header is sent, (b) `SampleBody` is ignored
    entirely, and (c) the request body byte-stream is empty.
    Carrying a `SampleBody` into a GET case is therefore
    silent dead code and a coverage-scope regression that the
    success-leg envelope assertion will not catch.
  - The 404-bearing-but-omitted-from-failure-leg pattern
    (declared in the spec, but not exercised because the
    per-tag opener convention reserves 404 → CodeNotFound for
    canonical by-id `*-one` peers) reappears here after eleven
    consecutive 404-absent POST peers (API-0353..API-0362).
    The convention is per-tag, not per-spec-response-set, so
    future settings/* GET getters that declare 404 should
    continue to inherit the harness default 401 → CodeAuth
    failure-leg mapping rather than promoting 404 →
    CodeNotFound.
  - `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`,
    and `goreleaser` remain not installed locally and continue
    to be documented as missing rather than silently skipped —
    same status as every prior settings/* coverage story.

    rule established at API-0246) and pre-reserves the slot
    `bb-cov-providers-0051` for any future regression test.
  - `ralph/prd.json` — flipped `passes: true` for API-0051.
  - `ralph/progress.txt` — this entry.
- Verification: `gofmt -w .`, `go mod tidy`, `go vet ./...`,
  `go test ./...`, `go test -race ./...` all green.
- Results: API coverage advances from 145 → 146 passing /
  462 total (316 remaining). The bitbucket/* tag now has its
  first covered operation; six bitbucket/* peers remain
  uncovered (`bitbucket-create`, `bitbucket-one`,
  `bitbucket-getBitbucketRepositories`,
  `bitbucket-getBitbucketBranches`,
  `bitbucket-testConnection`, `bitbucket-update`).
- Remaining risks / follow-ups:
  - Future bitbucket/* peers must each re-verify their own
    spec shapes per the API-0345..API-0363 forward-reference
    lesson. Three peers carry `*Id` query parameters
    (`bitbucket-one` 1 param, `*-getBitbucketRepositories` 1
    param, `*-getBitbucketBranches` 3 params) — those peers
    should consult API-0033 `application-readTraefikConfig`
    (query-shaped GET precedent), not this entry.
  - Three bitbucket/* peers are POST mutations
    (`bitbucket-create`, `bitbucket-testConnection`,
    `bitbucket-update`) with response set 200/400/401/403/500
    (the canonical mutation set, **without 404**). They should
    consult the cross-tag `*-create` / `*-update` precedents
    (API-0292 `project-create`, API-0297 `project-update`,
    API-0338 `server-create`) and re-derive each required
    field from `internal/api/data/openapi.json` rather than
    cargo-culting from sibling slugs.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **Tag-opener entries are the most expensive to author** —
    they need to enumerate every prior namespace
    (`admin-cov-*`, `ai-cov-*`, `app-cov-*`, `backup-cov-*`,
    `compose-cov-*`, `deployment-cov-*`, `org-cov-*`,
    `proj-cov-*`, `srv-cov-*`, `set-cov-*` so far) and
    explicitly call out which namespace the new tag inherits
    from, plus enumerate every forthcoming peer the new tag
    will spawn so future contributors know where to grep.
    Subsequent peers in the same tag are cheaper because they
    can back-reference their tag-opener.
  - **Compact tag prefix selection:** when picking a
    `<prefix>-cov-*` token base for a new tag, prefer the
    shortest unambiguous abbreviation (matching `ai`, `app`,
    `srv`, `org`, `proj`, `set`). For `bitbucket`, the
    two-letter `bb-` is unambiguous against every prior
    namespace — `bit-` would collide with `bitbucket` itself
    if a future feature needs a sub-namespace, and the full
    `bitbucket-cov-*` would violate the compact-prefix
    convention. Future tag-openers should similarly pick the
    shortest unambiguous abbreviation.
  - **Bitbucket-specific spec verification observation:** the
    `bitbucket` tag has seven operations split 3 GET / 4 POST.
    The three GETs all carry 404 in their response set; the
    four POSTs do not (canonical mutation 200/400/401/403/500
    set). This split is per-operation, not per-tag, and
    matches the cross-tag pattern observed at every prior
    roster — verify each operation's response set
    individually rather than assuming a tag-wide rule.
  - **Forward-reference lesson re-applied:** the next story
    in the bitbucket/* roster, API-0052 `bitbucket-create`,
    pivots onto a POST with a request body — do **not** assume
    the body shape mirrors any other tag's `*-create` solely
    because the slug matches; consult API-0292 / API-0338 for
    the flat scalar-only mutation precedent, then re-derive
    each required field from
    `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /bitbucket.create > post`.
  - `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`,
    and `goreleaser` remain not installed locally and continue
    to be documented as missing rather than silently skipped —
    same status as every prior tag-opener story.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0052 - Cover bitbucket-create (POST /bitbucket.create)
- What was implemented: per-operation contract coverage for the
  `bitbucket-create` POST mutation. This is the second bitbucket/*
  story (after API-0051 `bitbucket-bitbucketProviders` opened the
  tag with a parameter-free GET) and the **first mutation** in the
  bitbucket/* roster — therefore the entry that *opens* the
  `bb-cov-*` per-tag fixture-isolation namespace reserved by
  API-0051's design header.
- The operation is a parameter-free POST with a required
  `application/json` request body whose schema declares nine
  top-level scalar fields:
    * REQUIRED scalars (2): `authId`, `name`.
    * OPTIONAL scalars (7, all plain string): `bitbucketId`,
      `bitbucketUsername`, `bitbucketEmail`, `appPassword`,
      `apiToken`, `bitbucketWorkspaceName`, `gitProviderId`.
  No nested objects, no arrays, no enums, no `anyOf [string, null]`
  nullables — the **flattest mutation body in the cross-tag
  roster so far**, strictly stringly-typed. Response set
  200/400/401/403/500 (no 404 — canonical mutation set, matching
  every prior `*-create` peer: API-0002 `ai-create`, API-0040
  `backup-create`, API-0292 `project-create`, API-0338
  `server-create`).
- Files changed:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended a 154-line
    apiCoverageCase struct literal (entry #135, sorted by StoryID
    between API-0051 `bitbucket-bitbucketProviders` and API-0066
    `compose-cancelDeployment` per the diff-friendliness rule).
    Carries `SampleBody` populated per the every-optional-populated
    convention; fixture token base `bb-cov-create-0052` opens the
    `bb-cov-*` namespace and pre-reserves the slugs
    `bb-cov-getBranches-0053`, `bb-cov-getRepos-0054`,
    `bb-cov-one-0055`, `bb-cov-testConn-0056`,
    `bb-cov-update-0057` for the forthcoming bitbucket/* peers.
    Failure-leg defaults exercise the canonical 401 → CodeAuth
    mapping. The header comment explicitly forbids cargo-culting
    this entry's flat-string-only shape into the upcoming
    bitbucket/* mutation peers (API-0056 `bitbucket-testConnection`,
    API-0057 `bitbucket-update`) without per-operation re-derivation
    from the embedded spec.
  - `ralph/prd.json` — flipped API-0052 `passes` to `true`.
  - `ralph/progress.txt` — this entry.
- Verification run (from repo root):
  - `gofmt -w .`                           → clean.
  - `goimports -w .`                       → not installed
                                             (documented).
  - `go mod tidy`                          → clean (no diff).
  - `go vet ./...`                         → pass.
  - `go test ./...`                        → pass (10 packages ok).
  - `go test -race ./...`                  → pass (10 packages ok).
  - Focused: `go test ./internal/cli -run 'TestAPICoverage/API-0052'
    -v` → all five subtests green (registry, schema, manifest,
    success round-trip, representative failure).
- Results: API coverage advances from 146 → 147 passing /
  462 total (315 remaining). The bitbucket/* tag now has its
  first mutation covered alongside the GET kickoff; five
  bitbucket/* peers remain uncovered (API-0053..API-0057).
- Remaining risks or follow-ups: API-0053
  `bitbucket-getBitbucketBranches` (GET) is the next story by
  PRD ordering. Per the spec it carries **three query
  parameters** (the largest GET parameter count in the
  bitbucket/* tag), so the next contributor should consult
  API-0033 `application-readTraefikConfig` for the
  query-shaped GET precedent — **not this entry**, which is a
  flat-scalar-only POST mutation. Future contributors must
  re-verify each bitbucket/* peer's shape per the
  API-0345..API-0363 forward-reference lesson.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **Flat-string-only mutation sub-shape:** the bitbucket/*
    `*-create` peer is the first cross-tag mutation entry that
    is **strictly stringly-typed at the body's top level** —
    no number fields (unlike API-0338 `server-create`'s
    `port: number`), no enum-pinned scalars (unlike API-0338's
    `serverType: "deploy"`), and no `anyOf [string, null]`
    nullables (unlike API-0292 `project-create`'s `description`
    and API-0338's `description` / `sshKeyId`). This is a
    distinct sub-shape inside the broader flat-scalar-only
    mutation cohort and worth flagging as a precedent for any
    future POST that turns out to have only string scalars.
    The flat-string-only shape is **per-operation** and must
    not be assumed for the upcoming bitbucket/* mutation peers
    (API-0056 `bitbucket-testConnection`, API-0057
    `bitbucket-update`) without re-derivation.
  - **Secret-looking field names in mutation bodies:** when a
    Dokploy POST body carries fields whose names *look* like
    credentials (`appPassword`, `apiToken`, `bearerToken`, etc.)
    the fixture values must be obviously-fake (suffixed
    `-fixture` and prefixed with the per-case fixture token
    base) so a casual reader or future automated secret
    scanner can immediately tell they are coverage fixtures.
    The harness's `output.NewRedactor(flags.Token)` only
    scrubs the explicit `--token` value plus the well-known
    transport patterns (`Authorization:`, `X-Auth-Token:`,
    `?token=`); it does NOT match embedded JSON keys named
    `appPassword` / `apiToken`, so these literals round-trip
    through the wire body unchanged. Future mutation entries
    with credential-shaped optional scalars should follow the
    same `<token>-<fieldname>-fixture` literal convention.
  - **`@example.test` mailbox literal:** for body fields typed
    as email-shaped strings, RFC 6761 reserves
    `*.example.test` for documentation; `bitbucketEmail`
    carries `bb-cov-create-0052@example.test` so the fixture
    cannot be mistaken for a real mailbox. Future mutation
    entries with email-shaped scalars should follow the same
    `<token>@example.test` convention rather than a freeform
    string.
  - **Tag-mutation-opener entries:** authoring the first
    mutation in a tag is the second-most-expensive entry shape
    after the tag opener itself — it must (a) inherit the
    `<prefix>-cov-*` namespace from the tag opener, (b) enumerate
    every cross-tag flat-scalar-only mutation precedent
    (API-0292 / API-0338 in this case), (c) explicitly forbid
    cargo-culting from sibling tag namespaces, (d) re-derive
    every body field from the embedded spec rather than
    mirroring the cross-tag precedent's body shape, and
    (e) pre-reserve fixture-token slugs for every forthcoming
    peer in the same tag so the namespace stays diff-traceable.
    Subsequent mutations in the same tag are cheaper because
    they back-reference this entry.
  - **No 404 on `/bitbucket.create`** — canonical mutation
    response set, matching every prior `*-create` peer
    (API-0002 `ai-create`, API-0040 `backup-create`, API-0292
    `project-create`, API-0338 `server-create`). Per the
    per-tag opener convention reasserted at API-0051's
    bitbucket/* design header, 404 → CodeNotFound stays
    reserved for the canonical by-id peer API-0055
    `bitbucket-one`.
  - `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`,
    and `goreleaser` remain not installed locally and continue
    to be documented as missing rather than silently skipped —
    same status as every prior coverage story.

  API-0006 `ai-getAll`; no `SampleBody`/`SampleQuery`/

  462 total (314 remaining). The settings/* tag now has 14
  covered peers; the parameter-free `getDokploy*` GET
  sub-family has 2 members (API-0363, API-0364).

  (GET) is the next story by PRD ordering. It is **also**
  declared a GET in the PRD with responses
  200/400/401/403/404/500, so on shape it appears to
  continue the parameter-free GET sub-family — but the
  next contributor must re-verify against
  `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.getIp >
  get` per the forward-reference lesson before assuming
  any field is identical to this entry. Do not assume the
  parameter list, request body, or response set carries
  over solely because the verb prefix and adjacent slug
  match.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **Sub-family confirmation, not introduction.** API-0364
    is the *second* parameter-free `getDokploy*` GET
    after API-0363 opened the sub-family. The header
    comment leans on API-0363 as the canonical reference
    rather than re-explaining the cohort from scratch;
    the comment's job is to *confirm* the sub-family
    shape and pre-reserve the next slot, not to re-derive
    every wire-level invariant the prior peer already
    established.
  - **Failure-leg consistency across the GET sub-family.**
    Both API-0363 and API-0364 declare 404 in the spec
    yet both deliberately omit failure-leg overrides so
    the harness exercises the canonical 401 → CodeAuth
    path. The 404-bearing-but-unused rationale is
    identical (these are platform-info getters, not by-id
    peers) — future parameter-free `getDokploy*`
    additions should match this pattern unless the
    operation genuinely keys on a single resource id.
  - **Comment density discipline.** The API-0363 entry's
    forward-reference comment to API-0364 successfully
    pre-warned this iteration; the API-0364 comment
    pre-warns API-0365 with the same forward-reference
    + per-operation re-verification hedge. Keep the
    pattern: every entry forward-references its
    immediate successor by PRD ordering and forbids
    cargo-culting until the next contributor re-derives
    the spec shape.

    pattern: every Dokploy OpenAPI operation is a one-struct-literal
    append. Spec changes (registry, schema, manifest) flow from the
    embedded OpenAPI document; harness coverage flows from the
    `coveredAPIOperations` table. Adding a new story never requires

  exercises all five subtests (registry, schema, manifest,

  pass.

  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` (+API-0054 entry,
    StoryID-sorted between API-0053 `bitbucket-getBitbucketBranches`
    and API-0066 `compose-cancelDeployment`).
  - `ralph/prd.json` (`API-0054.passes` flipped to `true`).
  - `ralph/progress.txt` (this entry).

    all five subtests (registry, schema, manifest, success,

    and `goreleaser` remain not installed locally; documented
    as missing rather than silently skipped, matching the
    convention reasserted on every preceding API-XXXX coverage
    commit.
- API coverage advances from 150 to 151 passing / 462 total.
- Remaining risks / follow-ups:
  - The next bitbucket/* roster entry is API-0055
    `bitbucket-one` — the canonical by-id peer that finally
    consumes the long-reserved 404 → CodeNotFound failure leg
    that API-0051/0052/0053/0054 each deferred to it. Per the
    PRD it's a single-required-query-param GET keyed by
    `bitbucketId` (matching this entry's wire shape); the next
    contributor MUST re-verify against
    `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /bitbucket.one > get`
    before assuming any field carries over from API-0054. The
    eleven-flip body-axis history of the settings/* `clean*`
    sub-roster (API-0353..API-0362) demonstrates why
    per-operation re-verification stays mandatory across every
    family transition. Slug `bb-cov-one-0055` is reserved per
    the API-0052/0053 namespace plan, and crucially API-0055
    will be the **first** bitbucket/* entry to set
    `FailureStatus: 404` + `FailureCode: yerr.CodeNotFound`
    (every prior bitbucket/* entry has used the harness default
    401 → CodeAuth).
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **Single-required-only query GET is the simplest cell.**
    API-0054 establishes the simplest-possible query-bearing
    GET shape in the bitbucket/* coverage matrix: one required
    string scalar, no optional siblings, no request body. The
    harness fixture is one map entry. This is a structural
    counterpoint not just to API-0053's 3-param shape but to
    the broader query-shape spectrum across the registry —
    API-0035 `application-search` (11 optional, 0 required),
    API-0053 `bitbucket-getBranches` (2 required + 1 optional),
    API-0054 here (1 required + 0 optional). Future
    single-required-only query GETs in any tag (API-0055
    `bitbucket-one` is next in line) can grep this entry for
    the canonical handling.
  - **Every-optional-populated rule is a NO-OP when no optionals
    exist.** API-0010 / API-0014 / API-0249 / API-0292 / API-0297
    / API-0052 / API-0053 each populated optional fields
    alongside required ones so the wire envelope was
    exhaustive. API-0054 exercises a degenerate case of the
    same rule: when the spec declares no optional siblings,
    the minimum-required envelope IS the whole envelope. The
    rule is preserved by virtue of the spec, not by additional
    fixture work. This generalises the every-optional-populated
    rule from "always populate optionals" to "always exercise
    the full envelope per the spec".
  - **`bb-cov-*` namespace expansion continues.** API-0051
    reserved `bb-cov-providers-0051` (no fixture), API-0052
    consumed `bb-cov-create-0052-*-fixture` for body fields,
    API-0053 consumed `bb-cov-getBranches-0053-*` for query
    values, and API-0054 now consumes `bb-cov-getRepos-0054-*`.
    Forthcoming bitbucket/* peers (API-0055..API-0057) inherit
    the `bb-cov-*` namespace per the per-tag fixture-isolation
    rule established at API-0246 `organization-active`.
    The reserved slugs `bb-cov-one-0055`, `bb-cov-testConn-0056`,
    `bb-cov-update-0057` remain reserved with no collisions.
  - **No new harness code needed.** Confirms the API-XXXX
    coverage pattern: every Dokploy OpenAPI operation can be
    covered by appending exactly one struct literal to
    `coveredAPIOperations` — the registry, schema, manifest,
    success round-trip, and representative-failure assertions
    flow automatically through the shared harness. The OpenAPI
    registry is the single source of truth; adding a coverage
    case never requires touching the registry, manifest
    emitter, or schema emitter.

- Added api_coverage_test.go fixture for `settings-haveActivateRequests` (GET /settings.haveActivateRequests). 22nd entry on the settings/* roster, 8th in the parameter-free GET sub-family (API-0363, 0364, 0365, 0366, 0367, 0368, 0371 → 0372). Spec-verified against `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.haveActivateRequests > get`: zero parameters, no request body, responses 200/400/401/403/404/500, 200 schema is canonical empty `{}` with `additionalProperties: false`. Reservation slot: `set-cov-haveActivateRequests-0372`.
- **Forward-reference correction recorded.** The API-0371 hand-off comment predicted this story would land in the "absence of 404" branch (clustering with the no-body POST peers API-0354/0357/0358/0359/0370). Direct inspection of the spec falsified that prediction in the affirmative direction: 404 IS declared on the operation, so this entry stays under the canonical parameter-free GET shape established at API-0363..API-0368/API-0371. The falsified-404 prediction is now itself a canonical example for the slug-prefix-pattern-matching cautionary tale.
- Files changed:
  - internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go (added API-0372 fixture entry; new forward-reference comment points the next contributor at API-0373 settings-haveTraefikDashboardPortEnabled with explicit notes that the response/parameter/body axes must each be re-verified per-operation despite the matching `have*` slug prefix).
  - ralph/prd.json (set passes=true for API-0372).
  - ralph/progress.txt (this entry).
- Verification: `gofmt -l .` (clean), `go mod tidy` (clean), `go vet ./...` (clean), `go test ./...` (all packages PASS), `go test -race ./...` (all packages PASS). Focused `go test -run "TestAPICoverage/API-0372" -v ./internal/cli/...` — all five subtests (registry, schema, manifest, success, failure) pass. `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`, `goreleaser` not installed locally; documented as missing per the existing baseline.
- API coverage advances from 155 to 156 passing / 462 total.
- Remaining risks / follow-ups: API-0373 `settings-haveTraefikDashboardPortEnabled` is the natural next case under the `have*` slug prefix; the new forward-reference comment explicitly flags that the response-set axis (presence vs absence of 404), the parameter axis (zero vs one optional query), and the request-body axis (none vs JSON) must each be re-verified per-operation despite the matching slug prefix. Use the API-0371→API-0372 falsified-404 prediction as the cautionary precedent.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The first concrete falsified forward-reference prediction in this run (API-0371 → API-0372 on the 404 axis). Prior hand-off comments had hedged with "may diverge" language and been confirmed correct (API-0367 → API-0368, API-0370 → API-0371); this entry shows the rule earning its keep. Future contributors should treat any forward-reference clustering claim ("looks like the no-body POST peers", "looks like the 404-bearing GET sub-roster") as a hypothesis to verify, never as a substitute for inspection.
  - Boolean platform-state probes (`have*` slug prefix) live structurally alongside parameter-free platform-info getters (`get*` slug prefix) under the same canonical empty-object-200 / no-body / no-parameter shape. Future `is*`-prefixed peers (API-0375 settings-isCloud, API-0376 settings-isUserSubscribed) are likely candidates for the same shape, but per the falsified-prediction lesson must each be re-verified per-operation.
  - The parameter-free GET settings/* sub-roster pattern is now stable across eight entries (API-0363, API-0364, API-0365, API-0366, API-0367, API-0368, API-0371, API-0372). Diff against the prior entry remains purely the StoryID, OperationID, Path, slug-named reservation, sub-roster ordinal, and the design-rationale comments naming the right preceding peers and the right next operation.
  - The `coveredAPIOperations` slice's closing `}` followed by `// TestAPICoverage_RegistryInvariants` continues to be the canonical insertion point. The `Edit` tool with the prior entry's forward-reference comment as the `old_string` anchor reliably preserves the surrounding fixture intact.

    prefix appears in the settings/* GET cohort for the first time
    here. Populated `SampleQuery` with the slug-named literal
    `set-cov-readDirectories-0377` under the per-tag `set-cov-*`
    isolation namespace originally established by API-0246
    `organization-active`. `SuccessResponse: '{}'` matches the

  - `go test ./...` → all packages pass.
  - `go test -race ./...` → all packages pass.
  - `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`,
    and `goreleaser` remain not installed locally (unchanged from

  registry fired the registry-presence, schema-reachability,
  manifest-inclusion, success-leg, and failure-leg subtests for
  API-0377 with no extra production code required — Dokploy's GET
  with one OPTIONAL query parameter is fully exercised by the
  generic httptest harness.
- Remaining risks/follow-ups:
  - The next case in the settings/* roster per PRD ordering is
    API-0378 `settings-readMiddlewareTraefikConfig` (declared a GET
    in the PRD). Per the slug-prefix-is-not-shape lesson reasserted
    at API-0371..API-0377, two consecutive `read*` entries can sit
    on opposite sides of the parameter axis; per-operation spec
    re-verification is mandatory before assuming the parameter
    list, request body, or response set carries over.
- Learnings for future iterations:
  - **Slug pivot does not predict parameter-axis direction.** The
    API-0376 hand-off comment correctly flagged the `is*` → `read*`
    slug pivot as a potential shape inflection point, but the
    pivot itself only predicted that *something* might change —
    not which side of the parameter axis the new entry would land
    on. Direct spec inspection at
    `/settings.readDirectories > get` was required to settle the
    parameter axis (parameter-bearing). The per-operation
    re-verification rule kept the contributor from accidentally
    inheriting either the API-0376 parameter-free shape (because
    the verb stayed GET) or the API-0373 parameter-bearing shape
    (because both share the OPTIONAL `serverId` axis); only the
    spec settled which side this entry sits on.
  - **Parameter-bearing GET forwarding via `SampleQuery` is
    well-trodden.** The API-0369 / API-0373 precedent for
    populating `SampleQuery` with a slug-named literal under the
    per-tag `set-cov-*` namespace works without modification for
    API-0377 — the harness does not gate on `required`-ness, it
    forwards whatever `SampleQuery` carries (per the API-0077
    `compose-getTags` precedent, the API-0041
    `backup-listBackupFiles` precedent, the API-0369
    `settings-getTraefikPorts` precedent, and the API-0373
    `settings-haveTraefikDashboardPortEnabled` precedent).
  - **`{}` empty-success body remains the dominant settings/* GET
    convention.** Every prior settings/* GET peer (API-0363..
    API-0376) emits `{}` with `additionalProperties: false`; the
    pattern carries forward to API-0377 unchanged. The
    success-leg envelope assertion stays focused on
    `data.method` / `data.status` rather than payload projection.
  - **Append-only registry insertion stays robust against
    duplicates.** The `old_string` anchor matched the API-0376
    forward-reference comment's last six lines (the
    `parameter-axis flip-flop across API-0371..API-0376` paragraph
    plus the closing `},\n}` of `coveredAPIOperations`) — long
    enough to be unique against the 9k-line file, short enough to
    be readable. This insertion strategy continues to be the
    safest way to extend the registry without risking a
    mis-matching duplicate string elsewhere in the file.

    re-verification is mandatory before assuming the parameter list,
    request body, or response set carries over.
- Learnings for future iterations:
  - **The `read*` family straddles both sides of the parameter axis.**
    API-0377 (parameter-bearing, OPTIONAL `serverId`) followed by
    API-0378 (parameter-free) confirms the slug-prefix-is-not-shape
    lesson on consecutive entries with the same `read*` prefix. The
    semantic distinction tracks scope: `readDirectories` probes a host
    filesystem (optionally per-server) while
    `readMiddlewareTraefikConfig` returns the controller-owned global
    Traefik middleware configuration (platform-wide, no server scope).
    Treat this distinction as a hint, not a rule — the parameter axis
    is settled only by direct spec inspection.

    direction.** The API-0377 hand-off comment explicitly flagged that
    two consecutive `read*` entries can sit on opposite sides of the
    parameter axis; the spec resolved the divergence in the negative
    direction (parameter-free), validating the hedge. Continue writing
    forward-reference comments that name the axes that may flip rather
    than predicting the outcome — the per-operation re-verification
    rule is the load-bearing safety net.
  - **Parameter-free GET entries with empty-object 200 bodies are the
    most concise coverage shape.** Omitting `SampleQuery`,
    `SamplePathParams`, and `SampleBody` lets `buildCoverageInputArgs`
    return no `--input` flags, mirroring API-0363..API-0368, API-0371,
    API-0372, API-0374, API-0375, and API-0376 exactly. The success-leg
    path-method-status assertion remains exhaustive for this shape.
  - **Append-only registry insertion stays robust against duplicates.**
    The `old_string` anchor matched the API-0377 forward-reference
    comment's last six lines (the `parameter-axis flip-flop across
    API-0371..API-0377` paragraph plus the closing `},\n}` of
    `coveredAPIOperations`) — long enough to be unique against the
    10k-line file, short enough to be readable. This insertion strategy
    continues to be the safest way to extend the registry without
    risking a mis-matching duplicate string elsewhere in the file.

## 2026-05-08 - API-0379
- Implemented: Added settings-readTraefikConfig (GET
  /settings.readTraefikConfig) to the append-only
  `coveredAPIOperations` slice in `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go`.
  Spec re-verified per the API-0345..API-0378 forward-reference
  lesson against `internal/api/data/openapi.json >
  /settings.readTraefikConfig > get`: a GET with **zero parameters**
  (`parameters: null` in the embedded spec, materialized as
  `[]` in the PRD) and **no request body** (GETs in this OpenAPI
  document never carry a `requestBody` field). Responses
  200/400/401/403/404/500 — the 404 stays present, matching the
  parameter-free GET sub-roster opened at API-0363
  `settings-getDokployCloudIps` and extended through
  API-0364..API-0368, API-0371, API-0372, API-0374, API-0375,
  API-0376, and API-0378. The 200 schema is `{}` with
  `additionalProperties: false`, matching every prior covered peer
  in the settings/* GET cohort.
- Files changed:
  - `internal/cli/api_coverage_test.go` — appended a single
    `apiCoverageCase` literal (sixteenth entry of the settings/*
    GET cohort, thirteenth of the parameter-free GET sub-roster,
    twenty-ninth entry overall on the settings/* coverage
    roster). No production code changes — the case carries
    `StoryID`, `OperationID`, `Method`, `Path`, `Tag`, and
    `SuccessResponse: '{}'` only. `SampleQuery`,
    `SamplePathParams`, and `SampleBody` are intentionally
    omitted because `parameters: []` plus the no-body GET shape
    means there is zero fixture-bearing payload — the harness's
    `buildCoverageInputArgs` returns no `--input` flags and the
    success-leg path-method-status-only assertion remains
    exhaustive. Failure-leg fields are intentionally omitted: the
    per-tag opener convention reasserted at API-0335..API-0378
    reserves 404 → CodeNotFound for the canonical by-id `*-one`
    peer (which `readTraefikConfig` is not — it returns the
    controller-owned base Traefik configuration, no resource id
    to miss against). 401 → CodeAuth via the harness default
    (`tc.FailureStatus == 0` → 401, `tc.FailureCode == ""` →
    CodeAuth) is the universal-failure-mode representative.
  - `ralph/prd.json` — flipped `API-0379` `passes` to `true`.
- Verification run:
  - `gofmt -w .` → clean.
  - `go mod tidy` → clean.
  - `go vet ./...` → clean.
  - `go test ./...` → all packages pass.
  - `go test -race ./...` → all packages pass.
  - `goimports`, `golangci-lint`, `staticcheck`, `govulncheck`,
    and `goreleaser` remain not installed locally (unchanged from
    prior iterations).
- Results: All five required checks pass. The append-only coverage
  registry fired the registry-presence, schema-reachability,
  manifest-inclusion, success-leg, and failure-leg subtests for
  API-0379 with no extra production code required — Dokploy's
  parameter-free GET with the empty-object 200 schema is fully
  exercised by the generic httptest harness.
- Remaining risks/follow-ups:
  - 295 PRD stories still have `passes: false`. Next in PRD
    priority-2 settings/* ordering is API-0380
    `settings-readTraefikEnv` (declared a GET in the PRD). Per
    the slug-prefix-is-not-shape lesson reasserted at
    API-0371..API-0378, four consecutive `read*` entries
    (API-0377, API-0378, API-0379, and the upcoming API-0380)
    can still sit on different sides of the parameter axis or
    carry different response sets. The next contributor must
    re-verify the parameter axis, request-body axis, and
    response-set axis (presence/absence of 404) against
    `internal/api/data/openapi.json > /settings.readTraefikEnv >
    get` before assuming any field carries over from API-0379.
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - The `read*` slug-prefix family has now been observed three
    times consecutively (API-0377 `readDirectories`, API-0378
    `readMiddlewareTraefikConfig`, API-0379 `readTraefikConfig`)
    and the parameter axis has flipped within the family —
    parameter-bearing → parameter-free → parameter-free. This
    confirms the slug-prefix-is-not-shape lesson is **not** just
    an across-family rule; it applies **within** the same slug
    prefix. Two consecutive same-prefix alignments
    (API-0378→API-0379, both parameter-free) are NOT a
    substitute for per-operation spec re-verification — the rule
    still mandates inspection at every transition.
  - The base-Traefik-config family inside the settings/* roster
    (`readTraefikConfig`, `readMiddlewareTraefikConfig`, and the
    upcoming `readTraefikEnv`/`readTraefikFile`/
    `readWebServerTraefikConfig`) reads platform-wide
    controller-owned configuration, which structurally explains
    why these are parameter-free GETs: the controller is the
    sole authority on Traefik config, so no per-server scoping
    is needed (unlike `readDirectories` which probes a host
    filesystem and therefore optionally accepts `serverId`). The
    structural prediction does NOT obviate spec verification —
    `readTraefikEnv` etc. could still carry an OPTIONAL
    `serverId` if Dokploy decides to scope env reads
    per-worker. Direct spec inspection remains mandatory.
  - Diff against the prior parameter-free GET entry (API-0378)
    remains purely the StoryID, OperationID, Path, sub-roster
    ordinal, the design-rationale comments naming the right
    preceding peers, and the forward-reference comment naming
    the next operation. The append-only `coveredAPIOperations`
    slice plus the harness's
    `TestAPICoverage_RegistryInvariants`,
    `TestAPICoverage_SchemaCommandWorks`,
    `TestAPICoverage_ManifestListsOperation`,
    `TestAPICoverage_SuccessRoundTrip`, and
    `TestAPICoverage_RepresentativeFailure` mean a single struct
    literal exercises all eight acceptance criteria
    automatically.
  - The `Edit` tool's `old_string` anchor on the prior entry's
    forward-reference comment block (specifically the unique
    `API-0371..API-0378` parameter-axis flip-flop summary line)
    plus the closing `},\n}` of the slice continues to be the
    safest insertion strategy — the closing `},` of the prior
    entry is non-unique earlier in the file, but the
    forward-reference comment naming the next-but-one
    operation's PRD ID range is unique per story.

    confirm against the spec.
